import '../models/atlas_authenticity_evidence.dart';
import '../models/atlas_visual_inspection.dart';

final class AtlasAutographCoaInspectionEngine {
  const AtlasAutographCoaInspectionEngine();

  static const String id = 'atlas.scan.authenticity.autograph-coa';

  List<AtlasVisualEvidenceObservation> inspect({
    required String sessionId,
    required AtlasVisualInspection inspection,
    required DateTime observedAt,
  }) {
    final List<AtlasVisualEvidenceObservation> result =
        <AtlasVisualEvidenceObservation>[];

    final String signature = inspection.signature.trim();
    if (inspection.hasAutograph != null || signature.isNotEmpty) {
      result.add(
        AtlasVisualEvidenceObservation(
          observationId: 'scanner5c-$sessionId-autograph',
          sourceEngineId: id,
          area: AtlasInspectionArea.autograph,
          category: AtlasEvidenceCategory.autographCoa,
          label: 'Autograph observation',
          observedValue: signature.isEmpty
              ? 'Autograph presence recorded'
              : signature,
          confidence: inspection.resolvedEvidenceConfidence,
          scanImageId: _imageId(inspection, 'autograph'),
          attributes: <String, Object?>{
            if (inspection.hasAutograph != null)
              'hasAutograph': inspection.hasAutograph,
            if (signature.isNotEmpty) 'signature': signature,
            'providerVerified': false,
          },
          observedAt: observedAt,
        ),
      );
    }

    final String provider = inspection.authenticationProvider.trim();
    final String number = inspection.authenticityNumber.trim();
    if (inspection.hasCoa != null || provider.isNotEmpty || number.isNotEmpty) {
      result.add(
        AtlasVisualEvidenceObservation(
          observationId: 'scanner5c-$sessionId-coa',
          sourceEngineId: id,
          area: AtlasInspectionArea.coa,
          category: AtlasEvidenceCategory.autographCoa,
          label: 'COA / authentication observation',
          observedValue: <String>[
            if (provider.isNotEmpty) provider,
            if (number.isNotEmpty) number,
          ].join(' - '),
          confidence: inspection.resolvedEvidenceConfidence,
          scanImageId: _imageId(inspection, 'certification'),
          attributes: <String, Object?>{
            if (inspection.hasCoa != null) 'hasCoa': inspection.hasCoa,
            if (provider.isNotEmpty) 'provider': provider,
            if (number.isNotEmpty) 'certificateNumber': number,
            'providerVerified': false,
          },
          observedAt: observedAt,
        ),
      );
    }

    return List<AtlasVisualEvidenceObservation>.unmodifiable(result);
  }

  static String? _imageId(AtlasVisualInspection inspection, String typeName) {
    for (final image in inspection.images) {
      if (image.type.name == typeName) return image.imageId;
    }
    return null;
  }
}

FILE: .\lib\atlas\scan\authenticity\atlas_authenticity_rules.dart

import '../../intelligence/identity/atlas_identity_analysis_engine.dart';
import '../models/scan_session.dart';
import 'atlas_production_stamp_parser.dart';
import 'models/atlas_authenticity_assessment.dart';
import 'models/atlas_authenticity_evidence.dart';
import 'models/atlas_authenticity_signal.dart';
import 'models/atlas_inspection_reference.dart';
import 'models/atlas_production_stamp.dart';
import 'models/atlas_visual_inspection.dart';

abstract final class AtlasAuthenticityRules {
  static const double minimumEvidenceConfidence = 0.40;
  static const double categoryConcernCap = 0.35;
  static const double reviewThreshold = 0.10;
  static const double significantThreshold = 0.35;
  static const double strongIndicatorThreshold = 0.65;

  static AtlasAuthenticityAssessment evaluate({
    required ScanSession session,
    required AtlasVisualInspection inspection,
  }) {
    final List<AtlasAuthenticitySignal> displaySignals =
        <AtlasAuthenticitySignal>[];
    final List<AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal> evaluatedSignals =
        <AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal>[];
    final List<String> audit = <String>[];

    final AtlasInspectionReferenceCriteria reference =
        inspection.referenceCriteria ?? _fallbackReference(session);

    _evaluateLegacyInspection(
      session: session,
      inspection: inspection,
      reference: reference,
      displaySignals: displaySignals,
      evaluatedSignals: evaluatedSignals,
    );

    _evaluateStructuredObservations(
      inspection: inspection,
      reference: reference,
      evaluatedSignals: evaluatedSignals,
      displaySignals: displaySignals,
    );

    final double evidenceConfidence = inspection.resolvedEvidenceConfidence;
    final double inspectionCoverage =
        inspection.inspectedAreas.length / AtlasInspectionArea.values.length;
    final List<AtlasInspectionArea> uninspectedAreas = AtlasInspectionArea
        .values
        .where(
          (AtlasInspectionArea area) =>
              !inspection.inspectedAreas.contains(area),
        )
        .toList(growable: false);

    if (!inspection.hasAnyLegacyEvidence &&
        !inspection.hasStructuredObservations) {
      audit.add(
        'No inspection evidence was supplied. Uninspected areas do not count as discrepancies.',
      );
      return _assessment(
        concernLevel: AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.insufficientData,
        evidenceConfidence: 0.0,
        referenceCoverage: reference.completenessScore,
        inspectionCoverage: 0.0,
        aggregatedConcernScore: 0.0,
        summary:
            'There is not enough evidence to form a useful authenticity assessment.',
        displaySignals: displaySignals,
        evaluatedSignals: evaluatedSignals,
        uninspectedAreas: uninspectedAreas,
        audit: audit,
        productionStamp: inspection.hasProductionEvidence
            ? AtlasProductionStampParser.parse(inspection.productionStampText)
            : null,
      );
    }

    if (evidenceConfidence < minimumEvidenceConfidence) {
      audit.add(
        'Evidence confidence is below the assessment threshold. Low-quality evidence is not treated as counterfeit evidence.',
      );
      return _assessment(
        concernLevel: AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.insufficientData,
        evidenceConfidence: evidenceConfidence,
        referenceCoverage: reference.completenessScore,
        inspectionCoverage: inspectionCoverage,
        aggregatedConcernScore: 0.0,
        summary:
            'The available images or observations are not clear enough for a reliable authenticity comparison.',
        displaySignals: displaySignals,
        evaluatedSignals: evaluatedSignals,
        uninspectedAreas: uninspectedAreas,
        audit: audit,
        productionStamp: inspection.hasProductionEvidence
            ? AtlasProductionStampParser.parse(inspection.productionStampText)
            : null,
      );
    }

    if (!reference.canSupportConcernAssessment) {
      audit.add(
        'Reference coverage is insufficient for concern scoring. Incomplete reference data is not treated as counterfeit evidence.',
      );
      return _assessment(
        concernLevel: AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.insufficientData,
        evidenceConfidence: evidenceConfidence,
        referenceCoverage: reference.completenessScore,
        inspectionCoverage: inspectionCoverage,
        aggregatedConcernScore: 0.0,
        summary:
            'Atlas recorded the inspection evidence, but the reference data is not complete enough for a reliable authenticity comparison.',
        displaySignals: displaySignals,
        evaluatedSignals: evaluatedSignals,
        uninspectedAreas: uninspectedAreas,
        audit: audit,
        productionStamp: inspection.hasProductionEvidence
            ? AtlasProductionStampParser.parse(inspection.productionStampText)
            : null,
      );
    }

    final _ConcernAggregation aggregation = _aggregateConcern(
      evaluatedSignals,
      audit,
    );

    final AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel concernLevel;
    if (aggregation.criticalCategories >= 2 &&
        aggregation.score >= strongIndicatorThreshold) {
      concernLevel = AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.strongCounterfeitIndicators;
      audit.add(
        'Strong indicators require critical contradictions in at least two independent evidence categories.',
      );
    } else if (aggregation.score >= significantThreshold) {
      concernLevel = AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.significantInconsistencies;
    } else if (aggregation.score > reviewThreshold) {
      concernLevel = AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.reviewRecommended;
    } else {
      concernLevel = AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.noObviousConcerns;
    }

    return _assessment(
      concernLevel: concernLevel,
      evidenceConfidence: evidenceConfidence,
      referenceCoverage: reference.completenessScore,
      inspectionCoverage: inspectionCoverage,
      aggregatedConcernScore: aggregation.score,
      summary: _summaryForConcern(concernLevel),
      displaySignals: displaySignals,
      evaluatedSignals: evaluatedSignals,
      uninspectedAreas: uninspectedAreas,
      audit: audit,
      productionStamp: inspection.hasProductionEvidence
          ? AtlasProductionStampParser.parse(inspection.productionStampText)
          : null,
    );
  }

  static void _evaluateLegacyInspection({
    required ScanSession session,
    required AtlasVisualInspection inspection,
    required AtlasInspectionReferenceCriteria reference,
    required List<AtlasAuthenticitySignal> displaySignals,
    required List<AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal> evaluatedSignals,
  }) {
    final bool hasStructuredVariantSticker = inspection.observations.any(
      (AtlasVisualEvidenceObservation observation) =>
          observation.category == AtlasEvidenceCategory.variantSticker,
    );
    final bool hasStructuredProductionStamp = inspection.observations.any(
      (AtlasVisualEvidenceObservation observation) =>
          observation.category == AtlasEvidenceCategory.productionStamp,
    );
    final bool hasStructuredMacroPackaging = inspection.observations.any(
      (AtlasVisualEvidenceObservation observation) =>
          observation.category == AtlasEvidenceCategory.macroPackaging,
    );
    final bool hasStructuredAutographCoa = inspection.observations.any(
      (AtlasVisualEvidenceObservation observation) =>
          observation.category == AtlasEvidenceCategory.autographCoa,
    );

    if (inspection.hasStickerEvidence && !hasStructuredVariantSticker) {
      final analysis = AtlasIdentityAnalysisEngine.create().analyze(
        inspection.stickerText,
      );
      final Set<String> detected = <String>{
        ...analysis.stickerResult.stickers.map((value) => value.displayName),
        ...analysis.variantResult.variants.map((value) => value.displayName),
      };

      if (detected.isEmpty) {
        displaySignals.add(
          const AtlasAuthenticitySignal(
            id: 'sticker-unclassified',
            title: 'Sticker needs confirmation',
            message: 'Atlas could not classify the observed sticker text.',
            level: AtlasAuthenticitySignalLevel.caution,
            weight: -5,
          ),
        );
        evaluatedSignals.add(
          AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal(
            id: 'sticker-unclassified',
            area: AtlasInspectionArea.windowSticker,
            category: AtlasEvidenceCategory.variantSticker,
            severity: AtlasSignalSeverity.advisory,
            disposition: AtlasSignalDisposition.observational,
            confidence: inspection.resolvedEvidenceConfidence,
            baseWeight: 0.0,
            rationale:
                'The sticker text could not be classified. Unknown is not a discrepancy.',
          ),
        );
      } else {
        displaySignals.add(
          AtlasAuthenticitySignal(
            id: 'sticker-classified',
            title: 'Sticker evidence classified',
            message: detected.join(' â€¢ '),
            level: AtlasAuthenticitySignalLevel.support,
            weight: 10,
          ),
        );
      }

      final String expectedVariant =
          session.resolvedProduct?.variantName?.trim() ?? '';
      if (expectedVariant.isNotEmpty &&
          !inspection.stickerText.toLowerCase().contains(
            expectedVariant.toLowerCase(),
          )) {
        displaySignals.add(
          const AtlasAuthenticitySignal(
            id: 'sticker-variant-conflict',
            title: 'Sticker and catalog variant differ',
            message:
                'Confirm the exact sticker and physical variant before saving.',
            level: AtlasAuthenticitySignalLevel.warning,
            weight: -25,
          ),
        );
        evaluatedSignals.add(
          AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal(
            id: 'sticker-variant-conflict',
            area: AtlasInspectionArea.windowSticker,
            category: AtlasEvidenceCategory.variantSticker,
            severity: AtlasSignalSeverity.moderate,
            disposition: AtlasSignalDisposition.discrepancy,
            confidence: inspection.resolvedEvidenceConfidence,
            baseWeight: 0.25,
            rationale:
                'Observed sticker text does not contain the resolved catalog variant.',
          ),
        );
      }

      if (reference.legitimateVariantTypes.isNotEmpty &&
          !reference.legitimateVariantTypes.any(
            (String variant) => inspection.stickerText.toLowerCase().contains(
              variant.toLowerCase(),
            ),
          )) {
        evaluatedSignals.add(
          AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal(
            id: 'sticker-reference-mismatch',
            area: AtlasInspectionArea.windowSticker,
            category: AtlasEvidenceCategory.variantSticker,
            severity: AtlasSignalSeverity.moderate,
            disposition: AtlasSignalDisposition.discrepancy,
            confidence: inspection.resolvedEvidenceConfidence,
            baseWeight: 0.28,
            rationale:
                'Observed sticker text does not match a recorded legitimate variant family.',
          ),
        );
      }
    }

    if (inspection.hasProductionEvidence && !hasStructuredProductionStamp) {
      final stamp = AtlasProductionStampParser.parse(
        inspection.productionStampText,
      );
      if (stamp.isValid) {
        displaySignals.add(
          AtlasAuthenticitySignal(
            id: 'production-stamp',
            title: '${stamp.factoryCode} production stamp recognized',
            message: stamp.itemBatch ?? inspection.productionStampText,
            level: AtlasAuthenticitySignalLevel.support,
            weight: 10,
          ),
        );
      } else {
        displaySignals.add(
          const AtlasAuthenticitySignal(
            id: 'production-stamp-unreadable',
            title: 'Production stamp needs review',
            message:
                'The bottom-box marking is not a recognized JJL/FAC format.',
            level: AtlasAuthenticitySignalLevel.caution,
            weight: -8,
          ),
        );
        evaluatedSignals.add(
          AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal(
            id: 'production-stamp-unreadable',
            area: AtlasInspectionArea.bottomStamp,
            category: AtlasEvidenceCategory.productionStamp,
            severity: AtlasSignalSeverity.advisory,
            disposition: AtlasSignalDisposition.observational,
            confidence: inspection.resolvedEvidenceConfidence,
            baseWeight: 0.0,
            rationale:
                'An unreadable or unsupported stamp format is insufficient evidence, not a contradiction.',
          ),
        );
      }
    }

    if (!hasStructuredVariantSticker) {
      _addLegacyCheck(
        value: inspection.stickerPrintClear,
        id: 'sticker-print',
        area: AtlasInspectionArea.windowSticker,
        category: AtlasEvidenceCategory.variantSticker,
        goodTitle: 'Sticker print appears clear',
        warningTitle: 'Sticker print needs comparison',
        warningMessage:
            'Blurred or uneven printing can warrant closer comparison.',
        displaySignals: displaySignals,
        evaluatedSignals: evaluatedSignals,
        confidence: inspection.resolvedEvidenceConfidence,
      );
      _addLegacyCheck(
        value: inspection.stickerCutEven,
        id: 'sticker-cut',
        area: AtlasInspectionArea.windowSticker,
        category: AtlasEvidenceCategory.variantSticker,
        goodTitle: 'Sticker cut appears even',
        warningTitle: 'Sticker cut needs comparison',
        warningMessage:
            'An uneven border or cut can warrant closer comparison.',
        displaySignals: displaySignals,
        evaluatedSignals: evaluatedSignals,
        confidence: inspection.resolvedEvidenceConfidence,
      );
    }

    if (!hasStructuredMacroPackaging) {
      _addLegacyCheck(
        value: inspection.typographyConsistent,
        id: 'typography',
        area: AtlasInspectionArea.packagingCopyright,
        category: AtlasEvidenceCategory.macroPackaging,
        goodTitle: 'Macro typography appears consistent',
        warningTitle: 'Packaging typography needs comparison',
        warningMessage:
            'Large typography or layout differences should be reviewed, allowing for legitimate factory variance.',
        displaySignals: displaySignals,
        evaluatedSignals: evaluatedSignals,
        confidence: inspection.resolvedEvidenceConfidence,
      );
      _addLegacyCheck(
        value: inspection.borderConsistent,
        id: 'box-border',
        area: AtlasInspectionArea.packagingCopyright,
        category: AtlasEvidenceCategory.macroPackaging,
        goodTitle: 'Macro box layout appears consistent',
        warningTitle: 'Packaging layout needs comparison',
        warningMessage:
            'Large border or layout differences should be reviewed, allowing for legitimate manufacturing variance.',
        displaySignals: displaySignals,
        evaluatedSignals: evaluatedSignals,
        confidence: inspection.resolvedEvidenceConfidence,
      );
    }

    if (inspection.hasCopyrightEvidence && !hasStructuredMacroPackaging) {
      displaySignals.add(
        const AtlasAuthenticitySignal(
          id: 'copyright',
          title: 'Copyright evidence recorded',
          message: 'Copyright text is preserved for comparison and review.',
          level: AtlasAuthenticitySignalLevel.support,
          weight: 5,
        ),
      );
    }

    if (inspection.hasAutograph == true && !hasStructuredAutographCoa) {
      if (inspection.hasCoa == true && inspection.hasAuthenticationEvidence) {
        displaySignals.add(
          const AtlasAuthenticitySignal(
            id: 'autograph-documented',
            title: 'Autograph documentation recorded',
            message:
                'A COA provider or certification number was supplied. Atlas has not independently verified it.',
            level: AtlasAuthenticitySignalLevel.support,
            weight: 10,
          ),
        );
      } else {
        displaySignals.add(
          const AtlasAuthenticitySignal(
            id: 'autograph-unverified',
            title: 'Autograph verification not supplied',
            message:
                'The autograph was recorded without provider-verification evidence. This does not mean the autograph is invalid.',
            level: AtlasAuthenticitySignalLevel.caution,
            weight: 0,
          ),
        );
      }
      evaluatedSignals.add(
        AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal(
          id: 'autograph-observation',
          area: AtlasInspectionArea.autograph,
          category: AtlasEvidenceCategory.autographCoa,
          severity: AtlasSignalSeverity.info,
          disposition: AtlasSignalDisposition.observational,
          confidence: inspection.resolvedEvidenceConfidence,
          baseWeight: 0.0,
          rationale:
              'Autograph and COA data are observational in Scanner 5A and do not independently create counterfeit concern.',
        ),
      );
    }
  }

  static void _evaluateStructuredObservations({
    required AtlasVisualInspection inspection,
    required AtlasInspectionReferenceCriteria reference,
    required List<AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal> evaluatedSignals,
    required List<AtlasAuthenticitySignal> displaySignals,
  }) {
    for (final AtlasVisualEvidenceObservation observation
        in inspection.observations) {
      switch (observation.category) {
        case AtlasEvidenceCategory.productionStamp:
          final String? prefix =
              observation.attributes['factoryPrefix'] as String?;
          final DateTime? parsedDate =
              observation.attributes['parsedDate'] as DateTime?;
          if (prefix == null ||
              parsedDate == null ||
              reference.knownRuns.isEmpty) {
            evaluatedSignals.add(
              AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal(
                id: observation.observationId,
                area: observation.area,
                category: observation.category,
                severity: AtlasSignalSeverity.info,
                disposition: AtlasSignalDisposition.observational,
                confidence: observation.confidence,
                baseWeight: 0.0,
                rationale:
                    'Production evidence was recorded without enough structured reference data for a contradiction test.',
              ),
            );
            continue;
          }

          final bool matchesKnownRun = reference.knownRuns.any(
            (AtlasProductionRunReference run) =>
                run.matchesStamp(prefix: prefix, date: parsedDate),
          );
          evaluatedSignals.add(
            AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal(
              id: observation.observationId,
              area: observation.area,
              category: observation.category,
              severity: matchesKnownRun
                  ? AtlasSignalSeverity.info
                  : AtlasSignalSeverity.critical,
              disposition: matchesKnownRun
                  ? AtlasSignalDisposition.supporting
                  : AtlasSignalDisposition.discrepancy,
              confidence: observation.confidence,
              baseWeight: matchesKnownRun ? 0.0 : 0.50,
              rationale: matchesKnownRun
                  ? 'Production code aligns with a recorded manufacturing run.'
                  : 'Production code conflicts with recorded factory/date windows.',
            ),
          );
          break;

        case AtlasEvidenceCategory.variantSticker:
          final bool explicitCriticalMismatch =
              observation.attributes['criticalReferenceMismatch'] == true;
          final bool referenceMismatch =
              observation.attributes['referenceMismatch'] == true;
          final bool visualMismatch =
              observation.attributes['visualMismatch'] == true;
          final bool resolvedVariantMismatch =
              observation.attributes['resolvedVariantMismatch'] == true;

          if (explicitCriticalMismatch ||
              referenceMismatch ||
              visualMismatch ||
              resolvedVariantMismatch) {
            final AtlasSignalSeverity severity = explicitCriticalMismatch
                ? AtlasSignalSeverity.critical
                : referenceMismatch || resolvedVariantMismatch
                ? AtlasSignalSeverity.moderate
                : AtlasSignalSeverity.advisory;
            final double baseWeight = explicitCriticalMismatch
                ? 0.50
                : referenceMismatch || resolvedVariantMismatch
                ? 0.25
                : 0.12;
            evaluatedSignals.add(
              AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal(
                id: observation.observationId,
                area: observation.area,
                category: observation.category,
                severity: severity,
                disposition: AtlasSignalDisposition.discrepancy,
                confidence: observation.confidence,
                baseWeight: baseWeight,
                rationale: explicitCriticalMismatch
                    ? 'A specialized sticker engine reported a high-signal contradiction against vetted reference evidence.'
                    : referenceMismatch
                    ? 'Observed sticker or variant evidence differs from the recorded legitimate reference families.'
                    : resolvedVariantMismatch
                    ? 'Observed sticker or variant evidence differs from the resolved catalog variant and should be confirmed.'
                    : 'A visible sticker print or cut inconsistency was recorded for collector review.',
              ),
            );
          }
          break;

        case AtlasEvidenceCategory.macroPackaging:
          final bool explicitCriticalMismatch =
              observation.attributes['criticalReferenceMismatch'] == true;
          final bool macroMismatch =
              observation.attributes['macroMismatch'] == true;
          if (explicitCriticalMismatch || macroMismatch) {
            evaluatedSignals.add(
              AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal(
                id: observation.observationId,
                area: observation.area,
                category: observation.category,
                severity: explicitCriticalMismatch
                    ? AtlasSignalSeverity.critical
                    : AtlasSignalSeverity.advisory,
                disposition: AtlasSignalDisposition.discrepancy,
                confidence: observation.confidence,
                baseWeight: explicitCriticalMismatch ? 0.50 : 0.12,
                rationale: explicitCriticalMismatch
                    ? 'A specialized packaging engine reported a high-signal contradiction against vetted reference evidence.'
                    : 'A macro packaging difference was observed and should be reviewed with manufacturing variance in mind.',
              ),
            );
          }
          break;

        case AtlasEvidenceCategory.autographCoa:
          evaluatedSignals.add(
            AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal(
              id: observation.observationId,
              area: observation.area,
              category: observation.category,
              severity: AtlasSignalSeverity.info,
              disposition: AtlasSignalDisposition.observational,
              confidence: observation.confidence,
              baseWeight: 0.0,
              rationale:
                  'Autograph and COA observations are recorded without an automated authenticity conclusion.',
            ),
          );
          break;
      }
    }
  }

  static void _addLegacyCheck({
    required bool? value,
    required String id,
    required AtlasInspectionArea area,
    required AtlasEvidenceCategory category,
    required String goodTitle,
    required String warningTitle,
    required String warningMessage,
    required List<AtlasAuthenticitySignal> displaySignals,
    required List<AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal> evaluatedSignals,
    required double confidence,
  }) {
    if (value == null) return;

    if (value) {
      displaySignals.add(
        AtlasAuthenticitySignal(
          id: id,
          title: goodTitle,
          message: 'Collector-confirmed visual observation.',
          level: AtlasAuthenticitySignalLevel.support,
          weight: 5,
        ),
      );
      return;
    }

    displaySignals.add(
      AtlasAuthenticitySignal(
        id: id,
        title: warningTitle,
        message: warningMessage,
        level: AtlasAuthenticitySignalLevel.caution,
        weight: -8,
      ),
    );
    evaluatedSignals.add(
      AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal(
        id: id,
        area: area,
        category: category,
        severity: AtlasSignalSeverity.advisory,
        disposition: AtlasSignalDisposition.discrepancy,
        confidence: confidence,
        baseWeight: 0.12,
        rationale: warningMessage,
      ),
    );
  }

  static _ConcernAggregation _aggregateConcern(
    List<AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal> signals,
    List<String> audit,
  ) {
    final Map<AtlasEvidenceCategory, List<AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal>>
    grouped = <AtlasEvidenceCategory, List<AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal>>{};

    for (final AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal signal in signals) {
      if (!signal.isDiscrepancy) continue;
      grouped
          .putIfAbsent(
            signal.category,
            () => <AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal>[],
          )
          .add(signal);
    }

    double score = 0.0;
    int criticalCategories = 0;

    for (final MapEntry<
          AtlasEvidenceCategory,
          List<AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal>
        >
        entry
        in grouped.entries) {
      double raw = 0.0;
      bool hasCritical = false;
      for (final AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal signal in entry.value) {
        raw += signal.weightedConcern;
        hasCritical =
            hasCritical || signal.severity == AtlasSignalSeverity.critical;
      }

      if (hasCritical) {
        criticalCategories++;
      }

      final double clamped = raw.clamp(0.0, categoryConcernCap).toDouble();
      if (raw > categoryConcernCap) {
        audit.add(
          '${entry.key.name} concern was clamped from ${raw.toStringAsFixed(3)} to ${categoryConcernCap.toStringAsFixed(3)} to prevent correlated evidence from compounding.',
        );
      }
      score += clamped;
    }

    return _ConcernAggregation(
      score: score.clamp(0.0, 1.0).toDouble(),
      criticalCategories: criticalCategories,
    );
  }

  static AtlasInspectionReferenceCriteria _fallbackReference(
    ScanSession session,
  ) {
    final resolved = session.resolvedProduct;
    if (resolved == null) {
      return AtlasInspectionReferenceCriteria.unknown(
        productId: 'scan-${session.sessionId}',
      );
    }

    final List<String> variants = <String>[];
    if (resolved.variantName != null &&
        resolved.variantName!.trim().isNotEmpty) {
      variants.add(resolved.variantName!.trim());
    }

    return AtlasInspectionReferenceCriteria(
      productId: resolved.productId.value,
      upc: resolved.primaryBarcode,
      coverageLevel: AtlasReferenceCoverageLevel.limited,
      completenessScore: 0.35,
      legitimateVariantTypes: variants,
      provenance: <AtlasReferenceProvenance>[
        AtlasReferenceProvenance(
          sourceType: AtlasReferenceSourceType.inferredEstimate,
          sourceIdentifier: 'resolved-product-snapshot',
          lastVerifiedAt: DateTime.now().toUtc(),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  static AtlasAuthenticityAssessment _assessment({
    required AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel concernLevel,
    required double evidenceConfidence,
    required double referenceCoverage,
    required double inspectionCoverage,
    required double aggregatedConcernScore,
    required String summary,
    required List<AtlasAuthenticitySignal> displaySignals,
    required List<AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal> evaluatedSignals,
    required List<AtlasInspectionArea> uninspectedAreas,
    required List<String> audit,
    required AtlasProductionStamp? productionStamp,
  }) {
    final AtlasAuthenticityStatus status = switch (concernLevel) {
      AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.insufficientData =>
        AtlasAuthenticityStatus.insufficientEvidence,
      AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.reviewRecommended ||
      AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.significantInconsistencies ||
      AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.strongCounterfeitIndicators =>
        AtlasAuthenticityStatus.reviewRecommended,
      AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.noObviousConcerns =>
        inspectionCoverage >= 0.70
            ? AtlasAuthenticityStatus.strongEvidence
            : AtlasAuthenticityStatus.developingEvidence,
    };

    return AtlasAuthenticityAssessment(
      status: status,
      concernLevel: concernLevel,
      evidenceConfidence: evidenceConfidence,
      referenceCoverage: referenceCoverage,
      inspectionCoverage: inspectionCoverage,
      aggregatedConcernScore: aggregatedConcernScore,
      summary: summary,
      signals: displaySignals,
      evaluatedSignals: evaluatedSignals,
      uninspectedAreas: uninspectedAreas,
      auditLog: audit,
      productionStamp: productionStamp,
    );
  }

  static String _summaryForConcern(AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel concernLevel) {
    return switch (concernLevel) {
      AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.noObviousConcerns =>
        'No obvious concerns were found in the inspected evidence. This is not a guarantee of authenticity.',
      AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.reviewRecommended =>
        'One or more low-to-moderate inconsistencies should be reviewed before relying on this inspection.',
      AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.significantInconsistencies =>
        'Significant inconsistencies were found across the inspected evidence. Collector review is recommended.',
      AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.strongCounterfeitIndicators =>
        'Multiple independent high-signal contradictions were found. This is a warning assessment, not a definitive counterfeit determination.',
      AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.insufficientData =>
        'There is not enough reliable evidence or reference coverage to form an authenticity assessment.',
    };
  }
}

final class _ConcernAggregation {
  const _ConcernAggregation({
    required this.score,
    required this.criticalCategories,
  });

  final double score;
  final int criticalCategories;
}

import '../../models/scan_image.dart';
import 'atlas_authenticity_evidence.dart';
import 'atlas_inspection_reference.dart';

final class AtlasVisualInspection {
  AtlasVisualInspection({
    this.stickerText = '',
    this.productionStampText = '',
    this.copyrightText = '',
    this.signature = '',
    this.authenticationProvider = '',
    this.authenticityNumber = '',
    this.typographyConsistent,
    this.borderConsistent,
    this.stickerPrintClear,
    this.stickerCutEven,
    this.hasAutograph,
    this.hasCoa,
    this.notes = '',
    this.referenceCriteria,
    double? evidenceConfidence,
    List<AtlasVisualEvidenceObservation> observations =
        const <AtlasVisualEvidenceObservation>[],
    List<ScanImage> images = const <ScanImage>[],
  }) : evidenceConfidence = _normalizeOptionalConfidence(evidenceConfidence),
       observations = List<AtlasVisualEvidenceObservation>.unmodifiable(
         observations,
       ),
       images = List<ScanImage>.unmodifiable(images);

  final String stickerText;
  final String productionStampText;
  final String copyrightText;
  final String signature;
  final String authenticationProvider;
  final String authenticityNumber;
  final bool? typographyConsistent;
  final bool? borderConsistent;
  final bool? stickerPrintClear;
  final bool? stickerCutEven;
  final bool? hasAutograph;
  final bool? hasCoa;
  final String notes;
  final AtlasInspectionReferenceCriteria? referenceCriteria;
  final double? evidenceConfidence;
  final List<AtlasVisualEvidenceObservation> observations;
  final List<ScanImage> images;

  bool get hasStickerEvidence => stickerText.trim().isNotEmpty;
  bool get hasProductionEvidence => productionStampText.trim().isNotEmpty;
  bool get hasCopyrightEvidence => copyrightText.trim().isNotEmpty;
  bool get hasAuthenticationEvidence =>
      authenticationProvider.trim().isNotEmpty ||
      authenticityNumber.trim().isNotEmpty ||
      hasCoa != null;

  bool get hasStructuredObservations => observations.isNotEmpty;

  double get resolvedEvidenceConfidence {
    if (evidenceConfidence != null) {
      return evidenceConfidence!;
    }
    if (observations.isNotEmpty) {
      final double total = observations.fold<double>(
        0.0,
        (double sum, AtlasVisualEvidenceObservation observation) =>
            sum + observation.confidence,
      );
      return total / observations.length;
    }
    if (images.isNotEmpty) {
      return 0.85;
    }
    if (hasAnyLegacyEvidence) {
      return 0.65;
    }
    return 0.0;
  }

  bool get hasAnyLegacyEvidence {
    return hasStickerEvidence ||
        hasProductionEvidence ||
        hasCopyrightEvidence ||
        signature.trim().isNotEmpty ||
        authenticationProvider.trim().isNotEmpty ||
        authenticityNumber.trim().isNotEmpty ||
        typographyConsistent != null ||
        borderConsistent != null ||
        stickerPrintClear != null ||
        stickerCutEven != null ||
        hasAutograph != null ||
        hasCoa != null;
  }

  Set<AtlasInspectionArea> get inspectedAreas {
    final Set<AtlasInspectionArea> result = observations
        .map((AtlasVisualEvidenceObservation value) => value.area)
        .toSet();

    for (final ScanImage image in images) {
      switch (image.type) {
        case ScanImageType.frontBox:
          result.add(AtlasInspectionArea.frontBox);
        case ScanImageType.sticker:
          result.add(AtlasInspectionArea.windowSticker);
        case ScanImageType.bottomBox:
          result.add(AtlasInspectionArea.bottomStamp);
        case ScanImageType.backBox:
          result.add(AtlasInspectionArea.packagingCopyright);
        case ScanImageType.autograph:
          result.add(AtlasInspectionArea.autograph);
        case ScanImageType.certification:
          result.add(AtlasInspectionArea.coa);
        case ScanImageType.original:
        case ScanImageType.processed:
        case ScanImageType.thumbnail:
        case ScanImageType.barcode:
        case ScanImageType.condition:
        case ScanImageType.unknown:
          break;
      }
    }

    if (hasStickerEvidence ||
        stickerPrintClear != null ||
        stickerCutEven != null) {
      result.add(AtlasInspectionArea.windowSticker);
    }
    if (hasProductionEvidence) {
      result.add(AtlasInspectionArea.bottomStamp);
    }
    if (hasCopyrightEvidence ||
        typographyConsistent != null ||
        borderConsistent != null) {
      result.add(AtlasInspectionArea.packagingCopyright);
    }
    if (hasAutograph != null || signature.trim().isNotEmpty) {
      result.add(AtlasInspectionArea.autograph);
    }
    if (hasCoa != null || hasAuthenticationEvidence) {
      result.add(AtlasInspectionArea.coa);
    }

    return Set<AtlasInspectionArea>.unmodifiable(result);
  }

  static double? _normalizeOptionalConfidence(double? value) {
    if (value == null) {
      return null;
    }
    if (!value.isFinite || value < 0.0 || value > 1.0) {
      throw ArgumentError.value(
        value,
        'evidenceConfidence',
        'Evidence confidence must be between 0.0 and 1.0.',
      );
    }
    return value;
  }
}

import 'normalized_image_region.dart';

enum AtlasInspectionArea {
  frontBox,
  windowSticker,
  bottomStamp,
  packagingCopyright,
  autograph,
  coa,
}

enum AtlasEvidenceCategory {
  variantSticker,
  productionStamp,
  macroPackaging,
  autographCoa,
}

enum AtlasSignalSeverity { info, advisory, moderate, critical }

enum AtlasSignalDisposition { supporting, observational, discrepancy }

final class AtlasVisualEvidenceObservation {
  static const int currentSchemaVersion = 1;

  AtlasVisualEvidenceObservation({
    required String observationId,
    required String sourceEngineId,
    required this.area,
    required this.category,
    required String label,
    required String observedValue,
    required double confidence,
    String? scanImageId,
    this.cropRegion,
    Map<String, Object?> attributes = const <String, Object?>{},
    DateTime? observedAt,
  }) : observationId = _requireText(observationId, 'observationId'),
       sourceEngineId = _requireText(sourceEngineId, 'sourceEngineId'),
       label = _requireText(label, 'label'),
       observedValue = observedValue.trim(),
       confidence = _normalizeConfidence(confidence),
       scanImageId = _optionalText(scanImageId),
       attributes = _deepFreezeMap(attributes),
       observedAt = (observedAt ?? DateTime.now()).toUtc();

  final String observationId;
  final String sourceEngineId;
  final AtlasInspectionArea area;
  final AtlasEvidenceCategory category;
  final String label;
  final String observedValue;
  final double confidence;
  final String? scanImageId;
  final NormalizedImageRegion? cropRegion;
  final Map<String, Object?> attributes;
  final DateTime observedAt;

  Map<String, Object?> toJson() => <String, Object?>{
    'schemaVersion': currentSchemaVersion,
    'observationId': observationId,
    'sourceEngineId': sourceEngineId,
    'area': area.name,
    'category': category.name,
    'label': label,
    'observedValue': observedValue,
    'confidence': confidence,
    'scanImageId': scanImageId,
    'cropRegion': cropRegion?.toJson(),
    'attributes': attributes,
    'observedAt': observedAt.toIso8601String(),
  };

  static AtlasVisualEvidenceObservation? fromJson(Map<String, Object?> json) {
    try {
      if (json['schemaVersion'] != currentSchemaVersion) {
        return null;
      }

      final AtlasInspectionArea? area = _parseArea(json['area']);
      final AtlasEvidenceCategory? category = _parseCategory(json['category']);
      if (area == null || category == null) {
        return null;
      }

      NormalizedImageRegion? crop;
      final Object? rawCrop = json['cropRegion'];
      if (rawCrop != null) {
        if (rawCrop is! Map) {
          return null;
        }
        crop = NormalizedImageRegion.fromJson(
          Map<String, Object?>.from(rawCrop),
        );
        if (crop == null) {
          return null;
        }
      }

      final Object? rawAttributes = json['attributes'];
      final Map<String, Object?> attributes = rawAttributes is Map
          ? Map<String, Object?>.from(rawAttributes)
          : const <String, Object?>{};

      return AtlasVisualEvidenceObservation(
        observationId: json['observationId'] as String,
        sourceEngineId: json['sourceEngineId'] as String,
        area: area,
        category: category,
        label: json['label'] as String,
        observedValue: json['observedValue'] as String,
        confidence: (json['confidence'] as num).toDouble(),
        scanImageId: json['scanImageId'] as String?,
        cropRegion: crop,
        attributes: attributes,
        observedAt: DateTime.parse(json['observedAt'] as String).toUtc(),
      );
    } catch (_) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  static AtlasInspectionArea? _parseArea(Object? value) {
    if (value is! String) return null;
    for (final AtlasInspectionArea area in AtlasInspectionArea.values) {
      if (area.name == value) return area;
    }
    return null;
  }

  static AtlasEvidenceCategory? _parseCategory(Object? value) {
    if (value is! String) return null;
    for (final AtlasEvidenceCategory category in AtlasEvidenceCategory.values) {
      if (category.name == value) return category;
    }
    return null;
  }
}

final class AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal {
  AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal({
    required String id,
    required this.area,
    required this.category,
    required this.severity,
    required this.disposition,
    required double confidence,
    required double baseWeight,
    required String rationale,
  }) : id = _requireText(id, 'id'),
       confidence = _normalizeConfidence(confidence),
       baseWeight = _normalizeWeight(baseWeight),
       rationale = _requireText(rationale, 'rationale');

  final String id;
  final AtlasInspectionArea area;
  final AtlasEvidenceCategory category;
  final AtlasSignalSeverity severity;
  final AtlasSignalDisposition disposition;
  final double confidence;
  final double baseWeight;
  final String rationale;

  bool get isDiscrepancy => disposition == AtlasSignalDisposition.discrepancy;
  bool get isSupporting => disposition == AtlasSignalDisposition.supporting;
  double get weightedConcern => isDiscrepancy ? baseWeight * confidence : 0.0;
}

String _requireText(String value, String fieldName) {
  final String normalized = value.trim();
  if (normalized.isEmpty) {
    throw ArgumentError.value(value, fieldName, '$fieldName cannot be empty.');
  }
  return normalized;
}

String? _optionalText(String? value) {
  final String normalized = value?.trim() ?? '';
  return normalized.isEmpty ? null : normalized;
}

double _normalizeConfidence(double value) {
  if (!value.isFinite || value < 0.0 || value > 1.0) {
    throw ArgumentError.value(
      value,
      'confidence',
      'Confidence must be between 0.0 and 1.0.',
    );
  }
  return value;
}

double _normalizeWeight(double value) {
  if (!value.isFinite || value < 0.0) {
    throw ArgumentError.value(
      value,
      'baseWeight',
      'baseWeight must be finite and non-negative.',
    );
  }
  return value;
}

Map<String, Object?> _deepFreezeMap(Map<String, Object?> values) {
  final Map<String, Object?> copy = <String, Object?>{};
  for (final MapEntry<String, Object?> entry in values.entries) {
    copy[entry.key] = _deepFreeze(entry.value);
  }
  return Map<String, Object?>.unmodifiable(copy);
}

Object? _deepFreeze(Object? value) {
  if (value is Map) {
    final Map<String, Object?> mapped = <String, Object?>{};
    for (final MapEntry<Object?, Object?> entry in value.entries) {
      mapped[entry.key.toString()] = _deepFreeze(entry.value);
    }
    return Map<String, Object?>.unmodifiable(mapped);
  }
  if (value is List) {
    return List<Object?>.unmodifiable(value.map<Object?>(_deepFreeze));
  }
  if (value is Set) {
    return Set<Object?>.unmodifiable(value.map<Object?>(_deepFreeze));
  }
  return value;
}

import 'atlas_authenticity_evidence.dart';
import 'atlas_authenticity_signal.dart';
import 'atlas_production_stamp.dart';

enum AtlasAuthenticityStatus {
  strongEvidence,
  developingEvidence,
  reviewRecommended,
  insufficientEvidence,
}

enum AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel {
  noObviousConcerns,
  reviewRecommended,
  significantInconsistencies,
  strongCounterfeitIndicators,
  insufficientData,
}

final class AtlasAuthenticityAssessment {
  AtlasAuthenticityAssessment({
    required this.status,
    required this.concernLevel,
    required double evidenceConfidence,
    required double referenceCoverage,
    required double inspectionCoverage,
    required double aggregatedConcernScore,
    required this.summary,
    required List<AtlasAuthenticitySignal> signals,
    List<AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal> evaluatedSignals =
        const <AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal>[],
    List<AtlasInspectionArea> uninspectedAreas = const <AtlasInspectionArea>[],
    List<String> auditLog = const <String>[],
    this.productionStamp,
  }) : evidenceConfidence = _unitScore(
         evidenceConfidence,
         'evidenceConfidence',
       ),
       referenceCoverage = _unitScore(referenceCoverage, 'referenceCoverage'),
       inspectionCoverage = _unitScore(
         inspectionCoverage,
         'inspectionCoverage',
       ),
       aggregatedConcernScore = _unitScore(
         aggregatedConcernScore,
         'aggregatedConcernScore',
       ),
       signals = List<AtlasAuthenticitySignal>.unmodifiable(signals),
       evaluatedSignals = List<AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal>.unmodifiable(
         evaluatedSignals,
       ),
       uninspectedAreas = List<AtlasInspectionArea>.unmodifiable(
         uninspectedAreas,
       ),
       auditLog = List<String>.unmodifiable(auditLog);

  final AtlasAuthenticityStatus status;
  final AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel concernLevel;

  /// How clearly Atlas could observe the supplied evidence.
  final double evidenceConfidence;

  /// How complete/trustworthy the product reference criteria are.
  final double referenceCoverage;

  /// How much of the guided inspection surface was actually inspected.
  final double inspectionCoverage;

  /// Normalized concern score after category caps and correlation clamping.
  final double aggregatedConcernScore;

  final String summary;
  final List<AtlasAuthenticitySignal> signals;
  final List<AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal> evaluatedSignals;
  final List<AtlasInspectionArea> uninspectedAreas;
  final List<String> auditLog;
  final AtlasProductionStamp? productionStamp;

  // Backward-compatible accessors used by the existing engine/UI.
  int get confidenceScore =>
      (evidenceConfidence * 100).round().clamp(0, 100).toInt();
  int get evidenceCoverage =>
      (inspectionCoverage * 100).round().clamp(0, 100).toInt();

  bool get requiresReview =>
      concernLevel == AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.reviewRecommended ||
      concernLevel ==
          AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.significantInconsistencies ||
      concernLevel ==
          AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.strongCounterfeitIndicators ||
      concernLevel == AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.insufficientData;

  bool get hasWarnings => signals.any((AtlasAuthenticitySignal signal) {
    return signal.isWarning;
  });

  bool get hasEnoughReferenceData => referenceCoverage >= 0.50;

  static double _unitScore(double value, String name) {
    if (!value.isFinite || value < 0.0 || value > 1.0) {
      throw ArgumentError.value(
        value,
        name,
        '$name must be between 0.0 and 1.0.',
      );
    }
    return value;
  }
}

import 'package:google_mlkit_text_recognition/google_mlkit_text_recognition.dart';

abstract interface class AtlasTextRecognition {
  Future<String> recognize(String path);
  Future<void> close();
}

final class AtlasTextRecognitionService implements AtlasTextRecognition {
  AtlasTextRecognitionService({TextRecognizer? recognizer})
    : _recognizer = recognizer ?? TextRecognizer();

  final TextRecognizer _recognizer;

  @override
  Future<String> recognize(String path) async {
    final normalized = path.trim();
    if (normalized.isEmpty) return '';
    final result = await _recognizer.processImage(
      InputImage.fromFilePath(normalized),
    );
    return result.text.trim();
  }

  @override
  Future<void> close() => _recognizer.close();
}

// Describes the semantic meaning of OCR text recognized by Atlas.
//
// OCR engines should classify recognized text whenever possible so that
// downstream identity engines reason about structured knowledge instead of
// arbitrary strings.
enum OCRRecognitionType {
  unknown,

  // Core identity.
  productLine,
  brand,
  title,
  characterName,
  productNumber,
  series,

  // Variant information.
  sticker,
  exclusive,
  limitedEdition,
  chase,
  glow,
  metallic,
  flocked,
  diamond,
  blacklight,
  autograph,

  // Supporting information.
  certification,
  copyright,
  productionCode,
  manufacturer,
  language,
  date,
  website,
  barcodeText,
  qrText,
  miscellaneous,
}

extension OCRRecognitionTypeX on OCRRecognitionType {
  bool get isIdentity {
    return switch (this) {
      OCRRecognitionType.productLine ||
      OCRRecognitionType.brand ||
      OCRRecognitionType.title ||
      OCRRecognitionType.characterName ||
      OCRRecognitionType.productNumber ||
      OCRRecognitionType.series => true,
      _ => false,
    };
  }

  bool get isVariant {
    return switch (this) {
      OCRRecognitionType.sticker ||
      OCRRecognitionType.exclusive ||
      OCRRecognitionType.limitedEdition ||
      OCRRecognitionType.chase ||
      OCRRecognitionType.glow ||
      OCRRecognitionType.metallic ||
      OCRRecognitionType.flocked ||
      OCRRecognitionType.diamond ||
      OCRRecognitionType.blacklight ||
      OCRRecognitionType.autograph => true,
      _ => false,
    };
  }

  bool get displayPriority {
    return isIdentity || isVariant;
  }

  String get displayName {
    return switch (this) {
      OCRRecognitionType.unknown => 'Unknown',
      OCRRecognitionType.productLine => 'Product Line',
      OCRRecognitionType.brand => 'Brand',
      OCRRecognitionType.title => 'Title',
      OCRRecognitionType.characterName => 'Character Name',
      OCRRecognitionType.productNumber => 'Product Number',
      OCRRecognitionType.series => 'Series',
      OCRRecognitionType.sticker => 'Sticker',
      OCRRecognitionType.exclusive => 'Exclusive',
      OCRRecognitionType.limitedEdition => 'Limited Edition',
      OCRRecognitionType.chase => 'Chase',
      OCRRecognitionType.glow => 'Glow',
      OCRRecognitionType.metallic => 'Metallic',
      OCRRecognitionType.flocked => 'Flocked',
      OCRRecognitionType.diamond => 'Diamond',
      OCRRecognitionType.blacklight => 'Blacklight',
      OCRRecognitionType.autograph => 'Autograph',
      OCRRecognitionType.certification => 'Certification',
      OCRRecognitionType.copyright => 'Copyright',
      OCRRecognitionType.productionCode => 'Production Code',
      OCRRecognitionType.manufacturer => 'Manufacturer',
      OCRRecognitionType.language => 'Language',
      OCRRecognitionType.date => 'Date',
      OCRRecognitionType.website => 'Website',
      OCRRecognitionType.barcodeText => 'Barcode',
      OCRRecognitionType.qrText => 'QR Code',
      OCRRecognitionType.miscellaneous => 'Miscellaneous',
    };
  }
}

// Describes the role an image plays within an Atlas scan session.
enum ScanImageType {
  original,
  processed,
  thumbnail,
  frontBox,
  backBox,
  bottomBox,
  barcode,
  sticker,
  autograph,
  certification,
  condition,
  unknown,
}

// Describes the current processing state of a scan image.
enum ScanImageStatus {
  captured,
  queued,
  uploading,
  uploaded,
  processing,
  processed,
  approved,
  rejected,
  failed,
}

// Immutable image reference associated with a scan session.
//
// Images may initially exist only on the local device. Remote storage
// information can be added later after upload or synchronization.
class ScanImage {
  const ScanImage({
    required this.imageId,
    required this.type,
    required this.status,
    required this.capturedAt,
    this.localPath,
    this.remoteUrl,
    this.mimeType,
    this.width,
    this.height,
    this.byteSize,
    this.imageFingerprint,
    this.processingNotes = const [],
  });

  final String imageId;

  final ScanImageType type;

  final ScanImageStatus status;

  final DateTime capturedAt;

  final String? localPath;

  final String? remoteUrl;

  final String? mimeType;

  final int? width;

  final int? height;

  final int? byteSize;

  final String? imageFingerprint;

  final List<String> processingNotes;

  bool get hasLocalPath {
    return localPath != null && localPath!.trim().isNotEmpty;
  }

  bool get hasRemoteUrl {
    return remoteUrl != null && remoteUrl!.trim().isNotEmpty;
  }

  bool get hasFingerprint {
    return imageFingerprint != null && imageFingerprint!.trim().isNotEmpty;
  }

  bool get isAvailable {
    return hasLocalPath || hasRemoteUrl;
  }

  bool get isOriginal {
    return type == ScanImageType.original;
  }

  bool get isProcessed {
    return status == ScanImageStatus.processed ||
        status == ScanImageStatus.approved;
  }

  bool get requiresSync {
    return hasLocalPath && !hasRemoteUrl;
  }

  ScanImage copyWith({
    String? imageId,
    ScanImageType? type,
    ScanImageStatus? status,
    DateTime? capturedAt,
    String? localPath,
    String? remoteUrl,
    String? mimeType,
    int? width,
    int? height,
    int? byteSize,
    String? imageFingerprint,
    List<String>? processingNotes,
  }) {
    return ScanImage(
      imageId: imageId ?? this.imageId,
      type: type ?? this.type,
      status: status ?? this.status,
      capturedAt: capturedAt ?? this.capturedAt,
      localPath: localPath ?? this.localPath,
      remoteUrl: remoteUrl ?? this.remoteUrl,
      mimeType: mimeType ?? this.mimeType,
      width: width ?? this.width,
      height: height ?? this.height,
      byteSize: byteSize ?? this.byteSize,
      imageFingerprint: imageFingerprint ?? this.imageFingerprint,
      processingNotes: processingNotes ?? this.processingNotes,
    );
  }
}

import '../atlas/market/models/value_history_entry.dart';
import '../atlas/timeline/models/atlas_timeline_event.dart';

class Pop {
  final String id;

  final String name;
  final String number;

  final String category;
  final String series;

  final String edition;
  final String variant;
  final String retailExclusive;
  final String eventSticker;
  final String limitedEdition;
  final String signature;

  // External catalog identity.
  //
  // For HobbyDB imports:
  // sourceCatalogId = HDBID
  // sourceCollectibleId = HDBC Number
  // sourceUpc = UPC
  final String sourceCatalogId;
  final String sourceCollectibleId;
  final String sourceUpc;

  // Additional source metadata that helps Atlas preserve and explain
  // imported collectible releases.
  final String productionStatus;
  final String releaseDate;
  final String gradingCompany;
  final String authenticityNumber;

  // Physical release identity metadata.
  //
  // These fields describe meaningful release or packaging differences.
  // They do not describe the collector's individual copy condition.
  final String finish;
  final String size;
  final String packagingType;
  final String boxVersion;
  final String copyrightYear;
  final String productionCode;
  final String stickerType;
  final String authenticationProvider;

  final double value;
  final double pricePaid;

  // Chronological estimated-value records for this collectible.
  //
  // Legacy Pops may begin with a single synthesized history entry based on
  // their existing [value] and last known update date.
  final List<ValueHistoryEntry> valueHistory;

  // Chronological collector and market events for this collectible.
  final List<AtlasTimelineEvent> timeline;

  final int quantity;

  final String itemCondition;
  final String boxCondition;

  final String imageUrl;

  final String owner;

  final bool isWishlist;

  final String dateAdded;
  final String lastUpdated;

  const Pop({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.number,
    required this.category,
    required this.series,
    this.edition = 'Common',
    this.variant = 'Common',
    this.retailExclusive = 'None',
    this.eventSticker = 'None',
    this.limitedEdition = 'None',
    this.signature = 'Unsigned',
    this.sourceCatalogId = '',
    this.sourceCollectibleId = '',
    this.sourceUpc = '',
    this.productionStatus = '',
    this.releaseDate = '',
    this.gradingCompany = '',
    this.authenticityNumber = '',
    this.finish = 'Standard',
    this.size = 'Standard 4 Inch',
    this.packagingType = 'Single',
    this.boxVersion = '',
    this.copyrightYear = '',
    this.productionCode = '',
    this.stickerType = '',
    this.authenticationProvider = '',
    required this.value,
    required this.pricePaid,
    this.valueHistory = const [],
    this.timeline = const [],
    required this.quantity,
    required this.itemCondition,
    required this.boxCondition,
    required this.imageUrl,
    required this.owner,
    required this.isWishlist,
    this.dateAdded = '',
    this.lastUpdated = '',
  });

  factory Pop.fromJson(
      Map<String, dynamic> json,
      ) {
    final parsedId = json['id']?.toString() ?? '';
    final legacyEdition = json['edition'] ?? 'Common';

    final parsedValue = _readDouble(json['value']);
    final parsedDateAdded = json['dateAdded']?.toString() ?? '';
    final parsedLastUpdated = json['lastUpdated']?.toString() ?? '';

    final parsedValueHistory = _readValueHistory(
      json['valueHistory'],
      currentValue: parsedValue,
      dateAdded: parsedDateAdded,
      lastUpdated: parsedLastUpdated,
    );

    final parsedTimeline = _readTimeline(
      json['timeline'],
      popId: parsedId,
      dateAdded: parsedDateAdded,
    );

    return Pop(
      id: parsedId,
      name: json['name'] ?? '',
      number: json['number'] ?? '',
      category: json['category'] ?? '',
      series: json['series'] ?? '',
      edition: legacyEdition,
      variant: json['variant'] ?? legacyEdition,
      retailExclusive: json['retailExclusive'] ?? 'None',
      eventSticker: json['eventSticker'] ?? 'None',
      limitedEdition: json['limitedEdition'] ?? 'None',
      signature: json['signature'] ?? 'Unsigned',
      sourceCatalogId: json['sourceCatalogId'] ?? '',
      sourceCollectibleId: json['sourceCollectibleId'] ?? '',
      sourceUpc: json['sourceUpc'] ?? '',
      productionStatus: json['productionStatus'] ?? '',
      releaseDate: json['releaseDate'] ?? '',
      gradingCompany: json['gradingCompany'] ?? '',
      authenticityNumber: json['authenticityNumber'] ?? '',
      finish: json['finish'] ?? 'Standard',
      size: json['size'] ?? 'Standard 4 Inch',
      packagingType: json['packagingType'] ?? 'Single',
      boxVersion: json['boxVersion'] ?? '',
      copyrightYear: json['copyrightYear'] ?? '',
      productionCode: json['productionCode'] ?? '',
      stickerType: json['stickerType'] ?? '',
      authenticationProvider:
      json['authenticationProvider'] ?? '',
      value: parsedValue,
      pricePaid: _readDouble(json['pricePaid']),
      valueHistory: parsedValueHistory,
      timeline: parsedTimeline,
      quantity: _readInt(json['quantity'], fallback: 1),
      itemCondition: json['itemCondition'] ?? '',
      boxCondition: json['boxCondition'] ?? '',
      imageUrl: json['imageUrl'] ?? '',
      owner: json['owner'] ?? 'Shared',
      isWishlist: json['isWishlist'] ?? false,
      dateAdded: parsedDateAdded,
      lastUpdated: parsedLastUpdated,
    );
  }

  /// Identifies the exact collectible release itself for CONFIRMED duplicates.
  ///
  /// No single external identifier is trusted by itself.
  ///
  /// Catalog IDs and UPCs are strong identity evidence, but Funko releases may
  /// reuse UPCs or third-party catalogs may group multiple physical variants
  /// under one record. Release-defining attributes are therefore always included.
  String get collectibleIdentityKey {
    final catalogId = normalizeIdentityText(sourceCatalogId);
    final collectibleId = normalizeIdentityText(sourceCollectibleId);
    final upc = normalizeIdentityText(sourceUpc);

    final normalizedName = normalizeIdentityText(name);
    final normalizedNumber = normalizeIdentityText(number);
    final normalizedCategory = normalizeIdentityText(category);
    final normalizedSeries = normalizeIdentityText(series);

    final identityAnchor = _buildIdentityAnchor(
      catalogId: catalogId,
      collectibleId: collectibleId,
      upc: upc,
      normalizedName: normalizedName,
      normalizedNumber: normalizedNumber,
      normalizedCategory: normalizedCategory,
      normalizedSeries: normalizedSeries,
    );

    if (identityAnchor.isEmpty) {
      return '';
    }

    return <String>[
      'release',
      identityAnchor,
      ..._releaseIdentityParts,
    ].join('|');
  }

  /// Broad identity key used to group POSSIBLE duplicates for review.
  ///
  /// This intentionally ignores release-defining differences such as Chase,
  /// stickers, finishes, signatures, box versions, and production codes.
  ///
  /// Examples:
  ///
  /// Same UPC but Common vs Chase:
  ///   grouped for review, not confirmed as duplicates.
  ///
  /// Same number and series with slightly different names:
  ///   grouped for review.
  ///
  /// Same number in different Funko lines:
  ///   not grouped together.
  String get similarityReviewKey {
    final upc = normalizeIdentityText(sourceUpc);

    if (upc.isNotEmpty) {
      return 'upc_review|$upc';
    }

    final collectibleId = normalizeIdentityText(sourceCollectibleId);

    if (collectibleId.isNotEmpty) {
      return 'collectible_review|$collectibleId';
    }

    final catalogId = normalizeIdentityText(sourceCatalogId);

    if (catalogId.isNotEmpty) {
      return 'catalog_review|$catalogId';
    }

    final normalizedNumber = normalizeIdentityText(number);
    final normalizedCategory = normalizeIdentityText(category);
    final normalizedSeries = normalizeIdentityText(series);
    final normalizedName = normalizeIdentityText(name);

    if (normalizedNumber.isEmpty) {
      return '';
    }

    // Preferred manual review identity:
    // Number + Funko line/category + series.
    //
    // Name is intentionally omitted here so entries such as:
    // "Spider-Man" and "Spider Man"
    // can still be reviewed together.
    if (normalizedCategory.isNotEmpty && normalizedSeries.isNotEmpty) {
      return <String>[
        'line_review',
        normalizedNumber,
        normalizedCategory,
        normalizedSeries,
      ].join('|');
    }

    // When series is missing, retain the name to avoid grouping every #01
    // in a broad category together.
    if (normalizedCategory.isNotEmpty && normalizedName.isNotEmpty) {
      return <String>[
        'category_review',
        normalizedNumber,
        normalizedCategory,
        normalizedName,
      ].join('|');
    }

    // Last-resort review key. This is deliberately conservative.
    if (normalizedSeries.isNotEmpty && normalizedName.isNotEmpty) {
      return <String>[
        'series_review',
        normalizedNumber,
        normalizedSeries,
        normalizedName,
      ].join('|');
    }

    return '';
  }

  /// Builds the strongest available core identity anchor.
  ///
  /// The anchor identifies the probable catalog object. Physical release
  /// attributes are appended separately through [_releaseIdentityParts].
  String _buildIdentityAnchor({
    required String catalogId,
    required String collectibleId,
    required String upc,
    required String normalizedName,
    required String normalizedNumber,
    required String normalizedCategory,
    required String normalizedSeries,
  }) {
    if (collectibleId.isNotEmpty) {
      return 'collectible|$collectibleId';
    }

    if (catalogId.isNotEmpty) {
      return 'catalog|$catalogId';
    }

    if (upc.isNotEmpty) {
      return 'upc|$upc';
    }

    if (normalizedNumber.isEmpty) {
      return '';
    }

    // Number alone is never enough because every Funko line can restart at #01.
    if (normalizedCategory.isEmpty && normalizedSeries.isEmpty) {
      return '';
    }

    return <String>[
      'manual',
      normalizedName,
      normalizedNumber,
      normalizedCategory,
      normalizedSeries,
    ].join('|');
  }

  /// Release-defining attributes used by exact duplicate detection.
  ///
  /// Any meaningful difference here represents either:
  ///
  /// - another variant,
  /// - another sticker release,
  /// - another package format,
  /// - another box version,
  /// - another production run,
  /// - or another authenticated collectible.
  List<String> get _releaseIdentityParts {
    return <String>[
      normalizeVariantIdentity(edition),
      normalizeVariantIdentity(variant),
      normalizeVariantIdentity(finish),
      normalizeOptionalIdentity(retailExclusive),
      normalizeOptionalIdentity(eventSticker),
      normalizeOptionalIdentity(stickerType),
      normalizeOptionalIdentity(limitedEdition),
      normalizeSignatureIdentity(signature),
      normalizeOptionalIdentity(authenticationProvider),
      normalizeOptionalIdentity(gradingCompany),
      normalizeOptionalIdentity(authenticityNumber),
      normalizeOptionalIdentity(size),
      normalizeOptionalIdentity(packagingType),
      normalizeOptionalIdentity(boxVersion),
      normalizeOptionalIdentity(copyrightYear),
      normalizeOptionalIdentity(productionCode),
    ];
  }

  /// Identifies one stored record in a collection.
  ///
  /// Owner and wishlist status remain separate so different collectors and
  /// owned/wishlist records are never merged together.
  String get collectionRecordKey {
    final collectibleKey = collectibleIdentityKey;

    if (collectibleKey.isEmpty) {
      return '';
    }

    return [
      collectibleKey,
      normalizeIdentityText(owner),
      isWishlist ? 'wishlist' : 'owned',
    ].join('|');
  }

  String get csvImportKey => collectionRecordKey;

  String get duplicateKey => collectionRecordKey;

  String get collectorIdentity => collectibleIdentityKey;

  String get collectorLabel {
    final parts = <String>[
      variant,
      retailExclusive,
      eventSticker,
      limitedEdition,
      signature,
    ].where(hasMeaningfulIdentityLabel);

    if (parts.isEmpty) {
      return 'Common';
    }

    return parts.join(' â€¢ ');
  }

  Pop copyWith({
    String? id,
    String? name,
    String? number,
    String? category,
    String? series,
    String? edition,
    String? variant,
    String? retailExclusive,
    String? eventSticker,
    String? limitedEdition,
    String? signature,
    String? sourceCatalogId,
    String? sourceCollectibleId,
    String? sourceUpc,
    String? productionStatus,
    String? releaseDate,
    String? gradingCompany,
    String? authenticityNumber,
    String? finish,
    String? size,
    String? packagingType,
    String? boxVersion,
    String? copyrightYear,
    String? productionCode,
    String? stickerType,
    String? authenticationProvider,
    double? value,
    double? pricePaid,
    List<ValueHistoryEntry>? valueHistory,
    List<AtlasTimelineEvent>? timeline,
    int? quantity,
    String? itemCondition,
    String? boxCondition,
    String? imageUrl,
    String? owner,
    bool? isWishlist,
    String? dateAdded,
    String? lastUpdated,
  }) {
    return Pop(
      id: id ?? this.id,
      name: name ?? this.name,
      number: number ?? this.number,
      category: category ?? this.category,
      series: series ?? this.series,
      edition: edition ?? this.edition,
      variant: variant ?? this.variant,
      retailExclusive: retailExclusive ?? this.retailExclusive,
      eventSticker: eventSticker ?? this.eventSticker,
      limitedEdition: limitedEdition ?? this.limitedEdition,
      signature: signature ?? this.signature,
      sourceCatalogId: sourceCatalogId ?? this.sourceCatalogId,
      sourceCollectibleId:
      sourceCollectibleId ?? this.sourceCollectibleId,
      sourceUpc: sourceUpc ?? this.sourceUpc,
      productionStatus: productionStatus ?? this.productionStatus,
      releaseDate: releaseDate ?? this.releaseDate,
      gradingCompany: gradingCompany ?? this.gradingCompany,
      authenticityNumber:
      authenticityNumber ?? this.authenticityNumber,
      finish: finish ?? this.finish,
      size: size ?? this.size,
      packagingType: packagingType ?? this.packagingType,
      boxVersion: boxVersion ?? this.boxVersion,
      copyrightYear: copyrightYear ?? this.copyrightYear,
      productionCode: productionCode ?? this.productionCode,
      stickerType: stickerType ?? this.stickerType,
      authenticationProvider:
      authenticationProvider ?? this.authenticationProvider,
      value: value ?? this.value,
      pricePaid: pricePaid ?? this.pricePaid,
      valueHistory: valueHistory ?? this.valueHistory,
      timeline: timeline ?? this.timeline,
      quantity: quantity ?? this.quantity,
      itemCondition: itemCondition ?? this.itemCondition,
      boxCondition: boxCondition ?? this.boxCondition,
      imageUrl: imageUrl ?? this.imageUrl,
      owner: owner ?? this.owner,
      isWishlist: isWishlist ?? this.isWishlist,
      dateAdded: dateAdded ?? this.dateAdded,
      lastUpdated: lastUpdated ?? this.lastUpdated,
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'name': name,
      'number': number,
      'category': category,
      'series': series,
      'edition': edition,
      'variant': variant,
      'retailExclusive': retailExclusive,
      'eventSticker': eventSticker,
      'limitedEdition': limitedEdition,
      'signature': signature,
      'sourceCatalogId': sourceCatalogId,
      'sourceCollectibleId': sourceCollectibleId,
      'sourceUpc': sourceUpc,
      'productionStatus': productionStatus,
      'releaseDate': releaseDate,
      'gradingCompany': gradingCompany,
      'authenticityNumber': authenticityNumber,
      'finish': finish,
      'size': size,
      'packagingType': packagingType,
      'boxVersion': boxVersion,
      'copyrightYear': copyrightYear,
      'productionCode': productionCode,
      'stickerType': stickerType,
      'authenticationProvider': authenticationProvider,
      'value': value,
      'pricePaid': pricePaid,
      'valueHistory': valueHistory
          .map((entry) => entry.toJson())
          .toList(growable: false),
      'timeline': timeline
          .map((event) => event.toJson())
          .toList(growable: false),
      'quantity': quantity,
      'itemCondition': itemCondition,
      'boxCondition': boxCondition,
      'imageUrl': imageUrl,
      'owner': owner,
      'isWishlist': isWishlist,
      'dateAdded': dateAdded,
      'lastUpdated': lastUpdated,
    };
  }

  static double _readDouble(dynamic rawValue) {
    if (rawValue is num) {
      return rawValue.toDouble();
    }

    return double.tryParse(
      rawValue?.toString().trim() ?? '',
    ) ??
        0;
  }

  static int _readInt(
      dynamic rawValue, {
        required int fallback,
      }) {
    if (rawValue is int) {
      return rawValue;
    }

    if (rawValue is num) {
      return rawValue.toInt();
    }

    return int.tryParse(
      rawValue?.toString().trim() ?? '',
    ) ??
        fallback;
  }

  static List<ValueHistoryEntry> _readValueHistory(
      dynamic rawHistory, {
        required double currentValue,
        required String dateAdded,
        required String lastUpdated,
      }) {
    final entries = <ValueHistoryEntry>[];

    if (rawHistory is List) {
      for (final rawEntry in rawHistory) {
        if (rawEntry is! Map) {
          continue;
        }

        final entry = ValueHistoryEntry.fromJson(
          Map<String, dynamic>.from(rawEntry),
        );

        if (entry.value <= 0) {
          continue;
        }

        entries.add(entry);
      }
    }

    if (entries.isNotEmpty) {
      entries.sort(
            (a, b) => a.recordedAt.compareTo(b.recordedAt),
      );

      return List<ValueHistoryEntry>.unmodifiable(entries);
    }

    if (currentValue <= 0) {
      return const [];
    }

    final recordedAt =
        DateTime.tryParse(lastUpdated) ??
            DateTime.tryParse(dateAdded) ??
            DateTime.fromMillisecondsSinceEpoch(0);

    return List<ValueHistoryEntry>.unmodifiable(
      <ValueHistoryEntry>[
        ValueHistoryEntry(
          value: currentValue,
          recordedAt: recordedAt,
          source: ValueHistoryEntry.legacyValueSource,
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  static List<AtlasTimelineEvent> _readTimeline(
      dynamic rawTimeline, {
        required String popId,
        required String dateAdded,
      }) {
    final events = <AtlasTimelineEvent>[];

    if (rawTimeline is List) {
      for (final rawEvent in rawTimeline) {
        if (rawEvent is! Map) {
          continue;
        }

        final event = AtlasTimelineEvent.fromJson(
          Map<String, dynamic>.from(rawEvent),
        );

        events.add(event);
      }
    }

    if (events.isNotEmpty) {
      events.sort(
            (a, b) => a.occurredAt.compareTo(b.occurredAt),
      );

      return List<AtlasTimelineEvent>.unmodifiable(events);
    }

    final addedAt = DateTime.tryParse(dateAdded);

    if (popId.isEmpty || addedAt == null) {
      return const [];
    }

    return List<AtlasTimelineEvent>.unmodifiable(
      <AtlasTimelineEvent>[
        AtlasTimelineEvent(
          id: 'legacy-added-$popId',
          popId: popId,
          type: AtlasTimelineEventType.addedToVault,
          occurredAt: addedAt,
          title: 'Added to Vault',
          createdBy: AtlasTimelineEvent.systemCreatedBy,
          createdAt: addedAt,
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  // Cleans strings by lowercasing and stripping non-alphanumeric characters.
  // Converts "Spider-Man #01!" -> "spiderman01"
  static String normalizeIdentityText(String value) {
    return value
        .trim()
        .toLowerCase()
        .replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^a-z0-9]'), '');
  }

  static String normalizeVariantIdentity(String value) {
    final cleaned = normalizeIdentityText(value);

    if (cleaned.isEmpty ||
        cleaned == 'none' ||
        cleaned == 'no' ||
        cleaned == 'na' ||
        cleaned == 'common' ||
        cleaned == 'standard') {
      return 'common';
    }

    return cleaned;
  }

  static String normalizeOptionalIdentity(String value) {
    final cleaned = normalizeIdentityText(value);

    if (cleaned.isEmpty ||
        cleaned == 'none' ||
        cleaned == 'no' ||
        cleaned == 'na') {
      return 'none';
    }

    return cleaned;
  }

  static String normalizeSignatureIdentity(String value) {
    final cleaned = normalizeIdentityText(value);

    if (cleaned.isEmpty ||
        cleaned == 'none' ||
        cleaned == 'no' ||
        cleaned == 'na' ||
        cleaned == 'unsigned') {
      return 'unsigned';
    }

    return cleaned;
  }

  static bool hasMeaningfulIdentityLabel(String value) {
    final cleaned = normalizeIdentityText(value);

    return cleaned.isNotEmpty &&
        cleaned != 'none' &&
        cleaned != 'no' &&
        cleaned != 'na' &&
        cleaned != 'unsigned' &&
        cleaned != 'common' &&
        cleaned != 'standard';
  }
}
