import '../../../models/pop.dart';
import '../converters/atlas_catalog_converter.dart';
import '../models/atlas_catalog_product.dart';

// Central creation point for Vault Pop records.
//
// Screens, scanners, imports, catalog browsing, wishlist flows, and future
// Atlas Intelligence features should use this factory rather than constructing
// Pop records directly.
abstract final class AtlasPopFactory {
  // Creates a completely manual Vault record.
  //
  // This is used when no trusted Atlas catalog product is available.
  static Pop createManual({
    required String name,
    required String number,
    required String category,
    required String series,
    String variant = 'Common',
    String retailExclusive = 'None',
    String eventSticker = 'None',
    String limitedEdition = 'None',
    String signature = 'Unsigned',
    String productionCode = '',
    String copyrightYear = '',
    String stickerType = '',
    String authenticationProvider = '',
    String authenticityNumber = '',
    double value = 0,
    double pricePaid = 0,
    int quantity = 1,
    String itemCondition = '',
    String boxCondition = '',
    String imageUrl = '',
    String owner = 'Shared',
    bool isWishlist = false,
    DateTime? timestamp,
  }) {
    final DateTime resolvedTimestamp = timestamp ?? DateTime.now();

    final String now = resolvedTimestamp.toIso8601String();

    final String resolvedVariant = _defaultText(variant, fallback: 'Common');

    return Pop(
      id: resolvedTimestamp.microsecondsSinceEpoch.toString(),
      name: name.trim(),
      number: number.trim(),
      category: category.trim(),
      series: series.trim(),
      edition: resolvedVariant,
      variant: resolvedVariant,
      retailExclusive: _defaultText(retailExclusive, fallback: 'None'),
      eventSticker: _defaultText(eventSticker, fallback: 'None'),
      limitedEdition: _defaultText(limitedEdition, fallback: 'None'),
      signature: _defaultText(signature, fallback: 'Unsigned'),
      sourceCatalogId: '',
      sourceCollectibleId: '',
      sourceUpc: '',
      productionStatus: '',
      releaseDate: '',
      gradingCompany: '',
      productionCode: productionCode.trim(),
      copyrightYear: copyrightYear.trim(),
      stickerType: stickerType.trim(),
      authenticationProvider: authenticationProvider.trim(),
      authenticityNumber: authenticityNumber.trim(),
      value: value < 0 ? 0 : value,
      pricePaid: pricePaid < 0 ? 0 : pricePaid,
      quantity: quantity < 1 ? 1 : quantity,
      itemCondition: itemCondition.trim(),
      boxCondition: boxCondition.trim(),
      imageUrl: imageUrl.trim(),
      owner: _defaultText(owner, fallback: 'Shared'),
      isWishlist: isWishlist,
      dateAdded: now,
      lastUpdated: now,
    );
  }

  // Creates a Vault record from a trusted Atlas catalog product.
  //
  // The converter supplies catalog identity, barcode, image, value, and other
  // trusted metadata. Optional overrides allow the user to edit the form
  // before saving without losing the catalog connection.
  static Pop createFromCatalog({
    required AtlasCatalogProduct product,
    String? name,
    String? number,
    String? category,
    String? series,
    String? variant,
    String? retailExclusive,
    String? eventSticker,
    String limitedEdition = 'None',
    String? signature,
    String? productionCode,
    String? copyrightYear,
    String? stickerType,
    String? authenticationProvider,
    String? authenticityNumber,
    double? value,
    double pricePaid = 0,
    int quantity = 1,
    String itemCondition = '',
    String boxCondition = '',
    String? imageUrl,
    String owner = 'Shared',
    bool isWishlist = false,
    DateTime? timestamp,
  }) {
    final Pop catalogPop = AtlasCatalogConverter.toPop(
      product,
      owner: owner,
      isWishlist: isWishlist,
      quantity: quantity,
      pricePaid: pricePaid,
      itemCondition: itemCondition,
      boxCondition: boxCondition,
      timestamp: timestamp,
    );

    final String resolvedVariant = _defaultText(
      variant ?? catalogPop.variant,
      fallback: 'Common',
    );

    return catalogPop.copyWith(
      name: name?.trim() ?? catalogPop.name,
      number: number?.trim() ?? catalogPop.number,
      category: category?.trim() ?? catalogPop.category,
      series: series?.trim() ?? catalogPop.series,
      edition: resolvedVariant,
      variant: resolvedVariant,
      retailExclusive: retailExclusive == null
          ? catalogPop.retailExclusive
          : _defaultText(retailExclusive, fallback: 'None'),
      eventSticker: eventSticker == null
          ? catalogPop.eventSticker
          : _defaultText(eventSticker, fallback: 'None'),
      limitedEdition: _resolveCatalogIdentity(
        selectedValue: limitedEdition,
        catalogValue: catalogPop.limitedEdition,
        fallback: 'None',
      ),
      signature: signature == null
          ? catalogPop.signature
          : _defaultText(signature, fallback: 'Unsigned'),
      productionCode: productionCode?.trim() ?? catalogPop.productionCode,
      copyrightYear: copyrightYear?.trim() ?? catalogPop.copyrightYear,
      stickerType: stickerType?.trim() ?? catalogPop.stickerType,
      authenticationProvider:
          authenticationProvider?.trim() ?? catalogPop.authenticationProvider,
      authenticityNumber:
          authenticityNumber?.trim() ?? catalogPop.authenticityNumber,
      value: value == null
          ? catalogPop.value
          : value < 0
          ? 0
          : value,
      pricePaid: pricePaid < 0 ? 0 : pricePaid,
      quantity: quantity < 1 ? 1 : quantity,
      itemCondition: itemCondition.trim(),
      boxCondition: boxCondition.trim(),
      imageUrl: imageUrl?.trim() ?? catalogPop.imageUrl,
      owner: _defaultText(owner, fallback: 'Shared'),
      isWishlist: isWishlist,
    );
  }

  // Refreshes trusted catalog metadata on an existing Vault record.
  //
  // Personal ownership information remains protected by the converter.
  static Pop refreshFromCatalog({
    required Pop existingPop,
    required AtlasCatalogProduct product,
    DateTime? timestamp,
  }) {
    return AtlasCatalogConverter.applyCatalogMetadata(
      existingPop: existingPop,
      product: product,
      timestamp: timestamp,
    );
  }

  // Creates a wishlist record from a trusted Atlas catalog product.
  static Pop createWishlistFromCatalog({
    required AtlasCatalogProduct product,
    String owner = 'Shared',
    DateTime? timestamp,
  }) {
    return createFromCatalog(
      product: product,
      owner: owner,
      isWishlist: true,
      quantity: 1,
      timestamp: timestamp,
    );
  }

  static String _defaultText(String value, {required String fallback}) {
    final String normalized = value.trim();

    if (normalized.isEmpty) {
      return fallback;
    }

    return normalized;
  }

  static String _resolveCatalogIdentity({
    required String selectedValue,
    required String catalogValue,
    required String fallback,
  }) {
    final String normalizedSelection = selectedValue.trim();

    final bool selectionIsDefault =
        normalizedSelection.isEmpty ||
        normalizedSelection.toLowerCase() == 'none';

    if (!selectionIsDefault) {
      return normalizedSelection;
    }

    final String normalizedCatalogValue = catalogValue.trim();

    if (normalizedCatalogValue.isNotEmpty &&
        normalizedCatalogValue.toLowerCase() != 'none') {
      return normalizedCatalogValue;
    }

    return fallback;
  }
}

import 'dart:io';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../atlas/scan/acquisition/atlas_guided_inspection_capture_plan.dart';
import '../../atlas/scan/acquisition/atlas_inspection_image_capture_service.dart';
import '../../atlas/scan/authenticity/atlas_visual_inspection_service.dart';
import '../../atlas/scan/authenticity/models/atlas_visual_inspection.dart';
import '../../atlas/scan/models/scan_image.dart';
import '../../atlas/scan/models/scan_session.dart';
import '../../atlas/scan/recognition/atlas_text_recognition_service.dart';
import '../../theme/atlas_spacing.dart';
import '../../widgets/atlas/atlas_expandable_section.dart';
import '../../widgets/atlas/components/atlas_form_field.dart';

import '../../widgets/scanner/atlas_authenticity_card.dart';
import '../../widgets/vault/vault_scroll_view.dart';

class AtlasVisualInspectionScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const AtlasVisualInspectionScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.session,
    this.imageCapture,
    this.textRecognition,
    this.inspectionService = const AtlasVisualInspectionService(),
  });

  final ScanSession session;
  final AtlasInspectionImageCapture? imageCapture;
  final AtlasTextRecognition? textRecognition;
  final AtlasVisualInspectionService inspectionService;

  @override
  State<AtlasVisualInspectionScreen> createState() =>
      _AtlasVisualInspectionScreenState();
}

class _AtlasVisualInspectionScreenState
    extends State<AtlasVisualInspectionScreen> {
  final _sticker = TextEditingController();
  final _stamp = TextEditingController();
  final _copyright = TextEditingController();
  final _signature = TextEditingController();
  final _provider = TextEditingController();
  final _number = TextEditingController();
  final _notes = TextEditingController();

  late final AtlasInspectionImageCapture _capture;
  late final AtlasTextRecognition _recognizer;

  final List<ScanImage> _images = <ScanImage>[];
  bool? _typography;
  bool? _border;
  bool? _printClear;
  bool? _cutEven;
  bool? _autograph;
  bool? _coa;
  bool _busy = false;
  ScanSession? _preview;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _capture = widget.imageCapture ?? AtlasInspectionImageCaptureService();
    _recognizer = widget.textRecognition ?? AtlasTextRecognitionService();

    final existing = widget.session.visualInspectionContribution?.inspection;
    if (existing != null) {
      _sticker.text = existing.stickerText;
      _stamp.text = existing.productionStampText;
      _copyright.text = existing.copyrightText;
      _signature.text = existing.signature;
      _provider.text = existing.authenticationProvider;
      _number.text = existing.authenticityNumber;
      _notes.text = existing.notes;
      _images.addAll(existing.images);
      _typography = existing.typographyConsistent;
      _border = existing.borderConsistent;
      _printClear = existing.stickerPrintClear;
      _cutEven = existing.stickerCutEven;
      _autograph = existing.hasAutograph;
      _coa = existing.hasCoa;
    }

    if (widget.session.authenticityContribution != null) {
      _preview = widget.session;
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _sticker.dispose();
    _stamp.dispose();
    _copyright.dispose();
    _signature.dispose();
    _provider.dispose();
    _number.dispose();
    _notes.dispose();
    _recognizer.close();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _captureStep(
    AtlasGuidedInspectionStep step, {
    TextEditingController? textTarget,
  }) async {
    final source = await showModalBottomSheet<AtlasInspectionImageSource>(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => SafeArea(
        child: Wrap(
          children: [
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.photo_camera_outlined),
              title: const Text('Camera'),
              onTap: () =>
                  Navigator.pop(context, AtlasInspectionImageSource.camera),
            ),
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.photo_library_outlined),
              title: const Text('Gallery'),
              onTap: () =>
                  Navigator.pop(context, AtlasInspectionImageSource.gallery),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );

    if (source == null) return;

    setState(() => _busy = true);
    try {
      final ScanImage? image = await _capture.capture(
        source: source,
        type: step.imageType,
      );
      if (image == null) return;

      String recognizedText = '';
      if (step.supportsTextRecognition &&
          textTarget != null &&
          image.hasLocalPath) {
        recognizedText = await _recognizer.recognize(image.localPath!);
      }

      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _images.removeWhere((ScanImage item) => item.type == step.imageType);
        _images.add(image);
        if (recognizedText.isNotEmpty && textTarget != null) {
          textTarget.text = recognizedText;
        }
      });
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _busy = false);
    }
  }

  AtlasVisualInspection _inspection() => AtlasVisualInspection(
    stickerText: _sticker.text,
    productionStampText: _stamp.text,
    copyrightText: _copyright.text,
    signature: _signature.text,
    authenticationProvider: _provider.text,
    authenticityNumber: _number.text,
    typographyConsistent: _typography,
    borderConsistent: _border,
    stickerPrintClear: _printClear,
    stickerCutEven: _cutEven,
    hasAutograph: _autograph,
    hasCoa: _coa,
    notes: _notes.text,
    images: _images,
  );

  Future<void> _analyze({required bool finish}) async {
    setState(() => _busy = true);
    try {
      final ScanSession session = await widget.inspectionService.inspect(
        session: widget.session,
        inspection: _inspection(),
      );

      if (!mounted) return;
      if (finish) {
        Navigator.pop(context, session);
      } else {
        setState(() => _preview = session);
      }
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _busy = false);
    }
  }

  bool _hasImage(AtlasGuidedInspectionStep step) {
    return _images.any((ScanImage image) => image.type == step.imageType);
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final List<ScanImage> localPreviewImages = _images
        .where((ScanImage image) => image.hasLocalPath)
        .toList(growable: false);

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('Visual Inspection')),
      body: VaultScrollView(
        children: [
          const AtlasInfoBanner(
            title: 'Evidence, not certification',
            message:
                'Atlas records inspection evidence and warning signals. It does not certify an item as authentic or fake.',
            icon: Icons.info_outline_rounded,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.lg),
          _captureOnlySection(AtlasGuidedInspectionStep.frontBox),
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.lg),
          _textCaptureSection(
            step: AtlasGuidedInspectionStep.stickerWindow,
            controller: _sticker,
            label: 'Sticker Text',
            checks: [
              _check(

            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.lg),
          _textCaptureSection(
            step: AtlasGuidedInspectionStep.bottomStamp,
            controller: _stamp,
            label: 'Production Code (JJL/FAC)',
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.lg),
          AtlasExpandableSection(
            title: AtlasGuidedInspectionStep.packagingCopyright.title,
            icon: Icons.text_fields_rounded,
            initiallyExpanded: true,
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
              children: [
                Text(AtlasGuidedInspectionStep.packagingCopyright.guidance),
                const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.md),
                AtlasFormField(
                  controller: _copyright,
                  label: 'Copyright Text / Year',
                  icon: Icons.copyright_rounded,
                  maxLines: 3,

                const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.sm),
                _captureButton(
                  AtlasGuidedInspectionStep.packagingCopyright,
                  textTarget: _copyright,

                _check(
                  'Typography looks consistent',
                  _typography,
                  (bool? value) => _typography = value,
                ),
                _check(
                  'Box borders look consistent',
                  _border,
                  (bool? value) => _border = value,
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.lg),
          AtlasExpandableSection(
            title: 'âœï¸ Autograph & ðŸ“œ COA Evidence',
            icon: Icons.draw_outlined,
            initiallyExpanded: true,

              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,

                  _autograph,
                  (bool? value) => _autograph = value,
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.sm),

  Widget _textCaptureSection({
    required AtlasGuidedInspectionStep step,
    required TextEditingController controller,
    required String label,
    List<Widget> checks = const <Widget>[],

    return AtlasExpandableSection(
      title: step.title,
      icon: Icons.center_focus_strong_rounded,
      initiallyExpanded: true,
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          Text(step.guidance),
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.md),
          AtlasFormField(
            controller: controller,
            label: label,
            icon: Icons.text_snippet_outlined,
            maxLines: 3,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.sm),
          _captureButton(step, textTarget: controller),
          ...checks,
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _captureButton(
    AtlasGuidedInspectionStep step, {
    TextEditingController? textTarget,
  }) {
    final bool captured = _hasImage(step);
    return OutlinedButton.icon(
      onPressed: _busy
          ? null
          : () => _captureStep(step, textTarget: textTarget),
      icon: Icon(
        captured ? Icons.check_circle_outline : Icons.add_a_photo_outlined,
      ),
      label: Text(
        captured ? 'Captured â€” Replace Image' : 'Capture or Choose Image',
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _check(String label, bool? value, ValueChanged<bool?> update) {
    return DropdownButtonFormField<bool?>(
      initialValue: value,
      decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: label),
      items: const [
        DropdownMenuItem<bool?>(value: null, child: Text('Not inspected')),
        DropdownMenuItem<bool?>(value: true, child: Text('Yes')),
        DropdownMenuItem<bool?>(value: false, child: Text('No / warning seen')),
      ],
      onChanged: (bool? next) => setState(() => update(next)),
    );
  }

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';

import '../../atlas/engines/intelligence/atlas_insight_engine.dart';
import '../../atlas/engines/intelligence/atlas_recommendation_engine.dart';
import '../../atlas/services/atlas_activity_service.dart';
import '../../atlas/widgets/atlas_insight_card.dart';
import '../../constants/collector_identity_options.dart';
import '../../atlas/catalog/models/atlas_catalog_product.dart';
import '../../atlas/catalog/services/atlas_pop_factory.dart';
import '../../atlas/scan/authenticity/models/atlas_visual_inspection.dart';
import '../../services/app_state.dart';
import '../../theme/atlas_colors.dart';
import '../../theme/atlas_spacing.dart';
import '../../theme/atlas_typography.dart';
import '../../widgets/atlas/atlas_glass_card.dart';
import '../../widgets/atlas/atlas_glass_panel.dart';
import '../../widgets/atlas/atlas_expandable_section.dart';
import '../../widgets/atlas/components/atlas_dropdown_panel.dart';
import '../../widgets/atlas/components/atlas_form_field.dart';
import '../../widgets/atlas/components/atlas_product_image.dart';
import '../../widgets/atlas/components/atlas_section_header.dart';
import '../../widgets/pop/labeled_dropdown.dart';
import '../../widgets/pop/pop_form.dart';
import '../../widgets/vault/vault_scroll_view.dart';

class AddPopScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const AddPopScreen({
    super.key,
    this.atlasProduct,
    this.collectionName,
    this.visualInspection,
  });

  final AtlasCatalogProduct? atlasProduct;
  final String? collectionName;
  final AtlasVisualInspection? visualInspection;

  @override
  State<AddPopScreen> createState() => _AddPopScreenState();
}

class _AddPopScreenState extends State<AddPopScreen> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();

  final _nameController = TextEditingController();
  final _numberController = TextEditingController();
  final _categoryController = TextEditingController();
  final _seriesController = TextEditingController();
  final _valueController = TextEditingController();
  final _pricePaidController = TextEditingController();
  final _quantityController = TextEditingController(text: '1');
  final _itemConditionController = TextEditingController();
  final _boxConditionController = TextEditingController();
  final _imageUrlController = TextEditingController();
  final _customVariantController = TextEditingController();
  final _customRetailExclusiveController = TextEditingController();
  final _customEventStickerController = TextEditingController();
  final _customLimitedEditionController = TextEditingController();
  final _customSignatureController = TextEditingController();
  final _productionCodeController = TextEditingController();
  final _copyrightYearController = TextEditingController();
  final _stickerTypeController = TextEditingController();
  final _authenticationProviderController = TextEditingController();
  final _authenticityNumberController = TextEditingController();

  String _variant = 'Common';
  String _retailExclusive = 'None';
  String _eventSticker = 'None';
  String _limitedEdition = 'None';
  String _signature = 'Unsigned';

  bool get _hasCatalogProduct {
    return widget.atlasProduct != null;
  }

  String _formatCategory(String value) {
    if (value.isEmpty) {
      return '';
    }

    const upperCategories = {
      'dc': 'DC',
      'wwe': 'WWE',
      'nba': 'NBA',
      'nfl': 'NFL',
      'mlb': 'MLB',
      'ufc': 'UFC',
    };

    final lower = value.toLowerCase();

    if (upperCategories.containsKey(lower)) {
      return upperCategories[lower]!;
    }

    return value
        .split(' ')
        .map(
          (word) => word.isEmpty
              ? word
              : word[0].toUpperCase() + word.substring(1).toLowerCase(),
        )
        .join(' ');
  }

  String _catalogIdentitySelection({
    required String value,
    required List<String> options,
    required String fallback,
    required TextEditingController customController,
  }) {
    final String normalized = value.trim();

    if (normalized.isEmpty) {
      return fallback;
    }

    if (options.contains(normalized)) {
      return normalized;
    }

    customController.text = normalized;

    return 'Other';
  }

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();

    final AtlasCatalogProduct? product = widget.atlasProduct;

    final inspection = widget.visualInspection;
    if (inspection != null) {
      _productionCodeController.text = inspection.productionStampText;
      _copyrightYearController.text = inspection.copyrightText;
      _stickerTypeController.text = inspection.stickerText;
      _authenticationProviderController.text =
          inspection.authenticationProvider;
      _authenticityNumberController.text = inspection.authenticityNumber;
      if (inspection.signature.trim().isNotEmpty) {
        _customSignatureController.text = inspection.signature.trim();
        _signature = 'Other';
      }
    }

    if (product == null) {
      return;
    }

    final catalogPop = AtlasPopFactory.createFromCatalog(product: product);

    _nameController.text = catalogPop.name;
    _numberController.text = catalogPop.number;
    _categoryController.text = _formatCategory(catalogPop.category);
    _seriesController.text = catalogPop.series;
    _itemConditionController.text = catalogPop.itemCondition;
    _boxConditionController.text = catalogPop.boxCondition;
    _imageUrlController.text = catalogPop.imageUrl;

    if (catalogPop.value > 0) {
      _valueController.text = catalogPop.value.toStringAsFixed(2);
    }

    _variant = _catalogIdentitySelection(
      value: catalogPop.variant,
      options: CollectorIdentityOptions.variants,
      fallback: 'Common',
      customController: _customVariantController,
    );
    _retailExclusive = _catalogIdentitySelection(
      value: catalogPop.retailExclusive,
      options: CollectorIdentityOptions.retailExclusives,
      fallback: 'None',
      customController: _customRetailExclusiveController,
    );
    _eventSticker = _catalogIdentitySelection(
      value: catalogPop.eventSticker,
      options: CollectorIdentityOptions.eventStickers,
      fallback: 'None',
      customController: _customEventStickerController,
    );
    _limitedEdition = _catalogIdentitySelection(
      value: catalogPop.limitedEdition,
      options: CollectorIdentityOptions.limitedEditions,
      fallback: 'None',
      customController: _customLimitedEditionController,
    );
    _signature = _catalogIdentitySelection(
      value: catalogPop.signature,
      options: CollectorIdentityOptions.signatures,
      fallback: 'Unsigned',
      customController: _customSignatureController,
    );

    if (inspection != null && inspection.signature.trim().isNotEmpty) {
      _customSignatureController.text = inspection.signature.trim();
      _signature = 'Other';
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _nameController.dispose();
    _numberController.dispose();
    _categoryController.dispose();
    _seriesController.dispose();
    _valueController.dispose();
    _pricePaidController.dispose();
    _quantityController.dispose();
    _itemConditionController.dispose();
    _boxConditionController.dispose();
    _imageUrlController.dispose();
    _customVariantController.dispose();
    _customRetailExclusiveController.dispose();
    _customEventStickerController.dispose();
    _customLimitedEditionController.dispose();
    _customSignatureController.dispose();
    _productionCodeController.dispose();
    _copyrightYearController.dispose();
    _stickerTypeController.dispose();
    _authenticationProviderController.dispose();
    _authenticityNumberController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  String _resolvedIdentityValue({
    required String selectedValue,
    required TextEditingController customController,
    required String fallback,
  }) {
    if (selectedValue != 'Other') {
      return selectedValue;
    }

    final customValue = customController.text.trim();

    return customValue.isEmpty ? fallback : customValue;
  }

  Future<void> _savePop() async {
    if (!_formKey.currentState!.validate()) {
      return;
    }

    final resolvedVariant = _resolvedIdentityValue(
      selectedValue: _variant,
      customController: _customVariantController,
      fallback: 'Common',
    );

    final resolvedRetailExclusive = _resolvedIdentityValue(
      selectedValue: _retailExclusive,
      customController: _customRetailExclusiveController,
      fallback: 'None',
    );

    final resolvedEventSticker = _resolvedIdentityValue(
      selectedValue: _eventSticker,
      customController: _customEventStickerController,
      fallback: 'None',
    );

    final resolvedLimitedEdition = _resolvedIdentityValue(
      selectedValue: _limitedEdition,
      customController: _customLimitedEditionController,
      fallback: 'None',
    );

    final selectedSignature = _resolvedIdentityValue(
      selectedValue: _signature,
      customController: _customSignatureController,
      fallback: 'Unsigned',
    );
    final resolvedSignature = _customSignatureController.text.trim().isNotEmpty
        ? _customSignatureController.text.trim()
        : selectedSignature;

    final double value = PopFormValues.parseOptionalMoney(
      _valueController.text,
    );

    final double pricePaid = PopFormValues.parseOptionalMoney(
      _pricePaidController.text,
    );

    final int quantity = PopFormValues.parseQuantity(_quantityController.text);

    final AtlasCatalogProduct? product = widget.atlasProduct;

    final pop = product == null
        ? AtlasPopFactory.createManual(
            name: _nameController.text,
            number: _numberController.text,
            category: _formatCategory(_categoryController.text.trim()),
            series: _seriesController.text,
            variant: resolvedVariant,
            retailExclusive: resolvedRetailExclusive,
            eventSticker: resolvedEventSticker,
            limitedEdition: resolvedLimitedEdition,
            signature: resolvedSignature,
            productionCode: _productionCodeController.text,
            copyrightYear: _copyrightYearController.text,
            stickerType: _stickerTypeController.text,
            authenticationProvider: _authenticationProviderController.text,
            authenticityNumber: _authenticityNumberController.text,
            value: value,
            pricePaid: pricePaid,
            quantity: quantity,
            itemCondition: _itemConditionController.text,
            boxCondition: _boxConditionController.text,
            imageUrl: _imageUrlController.text,
            owner: 'Shared',
            isWishlist: false,
          )
        : AtlasPopFactory.createFromCatalog(
            product: product,
            name: _nameController.text,
            number: _numberController.text,
            category: _formatCategory(_categoryController.text.trim()),
            series: _seriesController.text,
            variant: resolvedVariant,
            retailExclusive: resolvedRetailExclusive,
            eventSticker: resolvedEventSticker,
            limitedEdition: resolvedLimitedEdition,
            signature: resolvedSignature,
            productionCode: _productionCodeController.text,
            copyrightYear: _copyrightYearController.text,
            stickerType: _stickerTypeController.text,
            authenticationProvider: _authenticationProviderController.text,
            authenticityNumber: _authenticityNumberController.text,
            value: value,
            pricePaid: pricePaid,
            quantity: quantity,
            itemCondition: _itemConditionController.text,
            boxCondition: _boxConditionController.text,
            imageUrl: _imageUrlController.text,
            owner: 'Shared',
            isWishlist: false,
          );

    final added = await context.read<AppState>().addPop(
      pop,
      collectionName: widget.collectionName,
    );

    if (!added) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          const SnackBar(
            content: Text(
              'This collection could not be found. The Pop was not added.',
            ),
          ),
        );
      }

      return;
    }

    AtlasActivityService.record(
      title: 'Pop Added',
      description: '${pop.name} #${pop.number} was added to the Vault.',
      type: 'pop_added',
    );

    if (!mounted) {
      return;
    }

    Navigator.pop(context, true);
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    const insightEngine = AtlasInsightEngine();

    final collectorInsight = insightEngine.buildCollectorIdentityInsight(
      variant: _variant,
      retailExclusive: _retailExclusive,
      eventSticker: _eventSticker,
      limitedEdition: _limitedEdition,
      signature: _signature,
    );

    const recommendationEngine = AtlasRecommendationEngine();

    final collectorRecommendation = recommendationEngine
        .buildCollectorIdentityRecommendation(
          variant: _variant,
          retailExclusive: _retailExclusive,
          eventSticker: _eventSticker,
          limitedEdition: _limitedEdition,
          signature: _signature,
          value: double.tryParse(_valueController.text) ?? 0,
          pricePaid: double.tryParse(_pricePaidController.text) ?? 0,
        );

    return Scaffold(
      resizeToAvoidBottomInset: true,
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('Add Funko Pop')),
      body: Form(
        key: _formKey,
        child: VaultScrollView(
          children: [
            AtlasGlassCard(
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text('Add Pop', style: AtlasTypography.headingLarge),
                  const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.sm),
                  Text(
                    'Add a new Pop to your current vault. Atlas will save the added date and updated date automatically.',
                    style: AtlasTypography.bodyMedium.copyWith(

                                Text(

                  AtlasFormField(

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';

import '../../atlas/engines/intelligence/atlas_insight_engine.dart';
import '../../atlas/engines/intelligence/atlas_recommendation_engine.dart';
import '../../models/activity.dart';
import '../../atlas/widgets/atlas_insight_card.dart';
import '../../constants/collector_identity_options.dart';
import '../../models/pop.dart';
import '../../services/app_state.dart';
import '../../theme/atlas_colors.dart';
import '../../theme/atlas_spacing.dart';
import '../../theme/atlas_typography.dart';
import '../../widgets/atlas/atlas_glass_card.dart';
import '../../widgets/atlas/atlas_glass_panel.dart';
import '../../widgets/atlas/atlas_expandable_section.dart';
import '../../widgets/atlas/components/atlas_dropdown_panel.dart';
import '../../widgets/atlas/components/atlas_form_field.dart';
import '../../widgets/atlas/components/atlas_product_image.dart';
import '../../widgets/atlas/components/atlas_section_header.dart';
import '../../widgets/pop/labeled_dropdown.dart';
import '../../widgets/pop/pop_form.dart';
import '../../widgets/vault/vault_scroll_view.dart';

enum EditPopInitialSection { none, collectorIdentity, condition, value }

class EditPopScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const EditPopScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.pop,
    this.collectionName,
    this.initialSection = EditPopInitialSection.none,
    this.focusValueField = false,
  });

  final Pop pop;
  final String? collectionName;

  /// Opens Edit Pop at a specific section.
  final EditPopInitialSection initialSection;

  /// Preserved for existing callers that specifically want the
  /// Estimated Value field selected and focused.
  final bool focusValueField;

  @override
  State<EditPopScreen> createState() => _EditPopScreenState();
}

class _EditPopScreenState extends State<EditPopScreen> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  final ScrollController _scrollController = ScrollController();

  final GlobalKey _collectorIdentitySectionKey = GlobalKey();
  final GlobalKey _conditionSectionKey = GlobalKey();
  final GlobalKey _valueSectionKey = GlobalKey();

  final FocusNode _itemConditionFocusNode = FocusNode();
  final FocusNode _valueFocusNode = FocusNode();

  late TextEditingController _nameController;
  late TextEditingController _numberController;
  late TextEditingController _categoryController;
  late TextEditingController _seriesController;
  late TextEditingController _valueController;
  late TextEditingController _pricePaidController;
  late TextEditingController _quantityController;
  late TextEditingController _itemConditionController;
  late TextEditingController _boxConditionController;
  late TextEditingController _imageUrlController;
  late TextEditingController _customVariantController;
  late TextEditingController _customRetailExclusiveController;
  late TextEditingController _customEventStickerController;
  late TextEditingController _customLimitedEditionController;
  late TextEditingController _customSignatureController;
  late TextEditingController _productionCodeController;
  late TextEditingController _copyrightYearController;
  late TextEditingController _stickerTypeController;
  late TextEditingController _authenticationProviderController;
  late TextEditingController _authenticityNumberController;

  late String _owner;
  late String _variant;
  late String _retailExclusive;
  late String _eventSticker;
  late String _limitedEdition;
  late String _signature;

  static const List<String> _defaultOwnerOptions = [
    'Arthur',
    'Natacia',
    'Amerika',
    'Nyssa',
    'Angelica',
    'Shared',
  ];

  late List<String> _ownerOptions;

  String _safeOption(String value, List<String> options, String fallback) {
    return options.contains(value) ? value : fallback;
  }

  bool _isCustomValue(String value, List<String> options) {
    final cleaned = value.trim();

    return cleaned.isNotEmpty && !options.contains(cleaned);
  }

  String _resolvedIdentityValue({
    required String selectedValue,
    required TextEditingController customController,
    required String fallback,
  }) {
    if (selectedValue != 'Other') {
      return selectedValue;
    }

    final customValue = customController.text.trim();

    return customValue.isEmpty ? fallback : customValue;
  }

  String _formatCategory(String value) {
    if (value.isEmpty) {
      return '';
    }

    const upperCategories = {
      'dc': 'DC',
      'wwe': 'WWE',
      'nba': 'NBA',
      'nfl': 'NFL',
      'mlb': 'MLB',
      'ufc': 'UFC',
    };

    final lower = value.toLowerCase();

    if (upperCategories.containsKey(lower)) {
      return upperCategories[lower]!;
    }

    return value
        .split(' ')
        .map(
          (word) => word.isEmpty
              ? word
              : word[0].toUpperCase() + word.substring(1).toLowerCase(),
        )
        .join(' ');
  }

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();

    _nameController = TextEditingController(text: widget.pop.name);
    _numberController = TextEditingController(text: widget.pop.number);
    _categoryController = TextEditingController(text: widget.pop.category);
    _seriesController = TextEditingController(text: widget.pop.series);
    _valueController = TextEditingController(text: widget.pop.value.toString());
    _pricePaidController = TextEditingController(
      text: widget.pop.pricePaid.toString(),
    );
    _quantityController = TextEditingController(
      text: widget.pop.quantity.toString(),
    );
    _itemConditionController = TextEditingController(
      text: widget.pop.itemCondition,
    );
    _boxConditionController = TextEditingController(
      text: widget.pop.boxCondition,
    );
    _imageUrlController = TextEditingController(text: widget.pop.imageUrl);
    _productionCodeController = TextEditingController(
      text: widget.pop.productionCode,
    );
    _copyrightYearController = TextEditingController(
      text: widget.pop.copyrightYear,
    );
    _stickerTypeController = TextEditingController(
      text: widget.pop.stickerType,
    );
    _authenticationProviderController = TextEditingController(
      text: widget.pop.authenticationProvider,
    );
    _authenticityNumberController = TextEditingController(
      text: widget.pop.authenticityNumber,
    );

    _owner = widget.pop.owner.trim().isEmpty
        ? 'Shared'
        : widget.pop.owner.trim();
    _ownerOptions = <String>[
      ..._defaultOwnerOptions,
      if (!_defaultOwnerOptions.contains(_owner)) _owner,
    ];

    final savedVariant = widget.pop.variant.trim().isNotEmpty
        ? widget.pop.variant.trim()
        : widget.pop.edition.trim();

    _customVariantController = TextEditingController(
      text: _isCustomValue(savedVariant, CollectorIdentityOptions.variants)
          ? savedVariant
          : '',
    );

    _variant = _isCustomValue(savedVariant, CollectorIdentityOptions.variants)
        ? 'Other'
        : _safeOption(
            savedVariant,
            CollectorIdentityOptions.variants,
            'Common',
          );

    _customRetailExclusiveController = TextEditingController(
      text:
          _isCustomValue(
            widget.pop.retailExclusive,
            CollectorIdentityOptions.retailExclusives,
          )
          ? widget.pop.retailExclusive
          : '',
    );

    _retailExclusive =
        _isCustomValue(
          widget.pop.retailExclusive,
          CollectorIdentityOptions.retailExclusives,
        )
        ? 'Other'
        : _safeOption(
            widget.pop.retailExclusive,
            CollectorIdentityOptions.retailExclusives,
            'None',
          );

    _customEventStickerController = TextEditingController(
      text:
          _isCustomValue(
            widget.pop.eventSticker,
            CollectorIdentityOptions.eventStickers,
          )
          ? widget.pop.eventSticker
          : '',
    );

    _eventSticker =
        _isCustomValue(
          widget.pop.eventSticker,
          CollectorIdentityOptions.eventStickers,
        )
        ? 'Other'
        : _safeOption(
            widget.pop.eventSticker,
            CollectorIdentityOptions.eventStickers,
            'None',
          );

    _customLimitedEditionController = TextEditingController(
      text:
          _isCustomValue(
            widget.pop.limitedEdition,
            CollectorIdentityOptions.limitedEditions,
          )
          ? widget.pop.limitedEdition
          : '',
    );

    _limitedEdition =
        _isCustomValue(
          widget.pop.limitedEdition,
          CollectorIdentityOptions.limitedEditions,
        )
        ? 'Other'
        : _safeOption(
            widget.pop.limitedEdition,
            CollectorIdentityOptions.limitedEditions,
            'None',
          );

    _customSignatureController = TextEditingController(
      text:
          _isCustomValue(
            widget.pop.signature,
            CollectorIdentityOptions.signatures,
          )
          ? widget.pop.signature
          : '',
    );

    _signature =
        _isCustomValue(
          widget.pop.signature,
          CollectorIdentityOptions.signatures,
        )
        ? 'Other'
        : _safeOption(
            widget.pop.signature,
            CollectorIdentityOptions.signatures,
            'Unsigned',
          );

    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      if (widget.focusValueField) {
        _focusValueSection();
        return;
      }

      _focusInitialSection();
    });
  }

  Future<void> _focusInitialSection() async {
    switch (widget.initialSection) {
      case EditPopInitialSection.none:
        return;

      case EditPopInitialSection.collectorIdentity:
        await _scrollToSection(_collectorIdentitySectionKey);
        return;

      case EditPopInitialSection.condition:
        await _scrollToSection(_conditionSectionKey);

        if (!mounted) {
          return;
        }

        _itemConditionFocusNode.requestFocus();
        return;

      case EditPopInitialSection.value:
        await _focusValueSection();
        return;
    }
  }

  Future<void> _scrollToSection(
    GlobalKey sectionKey, {
    double alignment = 0.12,
  }) async {
    if (!mounted) {
      return;
    }

    final BuildContext? sectionContext = sectionKey.currentContext;

    if (sectionContext == null) {
      return;
    }

    await Scrollable.ensureVisible(
      sectionContext,
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 450),
      curve: Curves.easeOutCubic,
      alignment: alignment,
    );
  }

  Future<void> _focusValueSection() async {
    await _scrollToSection(_valueSectionKey, alignment: 0.15);

    if (!mounted) {
      return;
    }

    _valueFocusNode.requestFocus();

    _valueController.selection = TextSelection(
      baseOffset: 0,
      extentOffset: _valueController.text.length,
    );
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _nameController.dispose();
    _numberController.dispose();
    _categoryController.dispose();
    _seriesController.dispose();
    _valueController.dispose();
    _pricePaidController.dispose();
    _quantityController.dispose();
    _itemConditionController.dispose();
    _boxConditionController.dispose();
    _imageUrlController.dispose();
    _scrollController.dispose();
    _itemConditionFocusNode.dispose();
    _valueFocusNode.dispose();
    _customVariantController.dispose();
    _customRetailExclusiveController.dispose();
    _customEventStickerController.dispose();
    _customLimitedEditionController.dispose();
    _customSignatureController.dispose();
    _productionCodeController.dispose();
    _copyrightYearController.dispose();
    _stickerTypeController.dispose();
    _authenticationProviderController.dispose();
    _authenticityNumberController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _saveChanges() async {
    if (!_formKey.currentState!.validate()) {
      return;
    }

    final resolvedVariant = _resolvedIdentityValue(
      selectedValue: _variant,
      customController: _customVariantController,
      fallback: 'Common',
    );

    final resolvedRetailExclusive = _resolvedIdentityValue(
      selectedValue: _retailExclusive,
      customController: _customRetailExclusiveController,
      fallback: 'None',
    );

    final resolvedEventSticker = _resolvedIdentityValue(
      selectedValue: _eventSticker,
      customController: _customEventStickerController,
      fallback: 'None',
    );

    final resolvedLimitedEdition = _resolvedIdentityValue(
      selectedValue: _limitedEdition,
      customController: _customLimitedEditionController,
      fallback: 'None',
    );

    final selectedSignature = _resolvedIdentityValue(
      selectedValue: _signature,
      customController: _customSignatureController,
      fallback: 'Unsigned',
    );
    final resolvedSignature = _customSignatureController.text.trim().isNotEmpty
        ? _customSignatureController.text.trim()
        : selectedSignature;

    final updatedPop = widget.pop.copyWith(
      name: _nameController.text.trim(),
      number: _numberController.text.trim(),
      category: _formatCategory(_categoryController.text.trim()),
      series: _seriesController.text.trim(),
      variant: resolvedVariant,
      retailExclusive: resolvedRetailExclusive,
      eventSticker: resolvedEventSticker,
      limitedEdition: resolvedLimitedEdition,
      signature: resolvedSignature,
      productionCode: _productionCodeController.text.trim(),
      copyrightYear: _copyrightYearController.text.trim(),
      stickerType: _stickerTypeController.text.trim(),
      authenticationProvider: _authenticationProviderController.text.trim(),
      authenticityNumber: _authenticityNumberController.text.trim(),
      value: PopFormValues.parseOptionalMoney(_valueController.text),
      pricePaid: PopFormValues.parseOptionalMoney(_pricePaidController.text),
      quantity: PopFormValues.parseQuantity(_quantityController.text),
      itemCondition: _itemConditionController.text.trim(),
      boxCondition: _boxConditionController.text.trim(),
      imageUrl: _imageUrlController.text.trim(),
      owner: _owner,
    );

    final appState = context.read<AppState>();
    final valueChanged = updatedPop.value != widget.pop.value;

    final persistedPop = await appState.updatePop(
      updatedPop,
      collectionName: widget.collectionName,
      existingRecordKey: widget.pop.collectionRecordKey,
    );

    if (persistedPop == null) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          const SnackBar(
            content: Text(
              'This Pop could not be found. No changes were saved.',
            ),
          ),
        );
      }

      return;
    }

    await appState.addActivity(
      Activity(
        id: DateTime.now().microsecondsSinceEpoch.toString(),
        popId: persistedPop.id,
        action: valueChanged ? 'value_updated' : 'pop_updated',
        popName: persistedPop.name,
        popNumber: persistedPop.number,
        owner: persistedPop.owner,
        actor: persistedPop.owner,
        details: valueChanged
            ? _valueUpdateDetails(
                pop: persistedPop,
                previousValue: widget.pop.value,
              )
            : persistedPop.number.trim().isEmpty
            ? '${persistedPop.name} was updated'
            : '${persistedPop.name} #${persistedPop.number} was updated',
        timestamp: DateTime.now(),
      ),
    );

    if (!mounted) {
      return;
    }

    Navigator.pop(context, persistedPop);
  }

  String _valueUpdateDetails({
    required Pop pop,
    required double previousValue,
  }) {
    final previousLabel = previousValue <= 0
        ? 'not entered'
        : '\$${previousValue.toStringAsFixed(2)}';

    final updatedLabel = pop.value <= 0
        ? 'not entered'
        : '\$${pop.value.toStringAsFixed(2)}';

    return '${pop.name} value changed from $previousLabel to $updatedLabel';
  }

  @override

    const insightEngine = AtlasInsightEngine();

    final collectorInsight = insightEngine.buildCollectorIdentityInsight(
      variant: _variant,
      retailExclusive: _retailExclusive,
      eventSticker: _eventSticker,
      limitedEdition: _limitedEdition,
      signature: _signature,
    );

    const recommendationEngine = AtlasRecommendationEngine();

    final collectorRecommendation = recommendationEngine
        .buildCollectorIdentityRecommendation(
          variant: _variant,
          retailExclusive: _retailExclusive,
          eventSticker: _eventSticker,
          limitedEdition: _limitedEdition,
          signature: _signature,
          value: double.tryParse(_valueController.text) ?? 0,
          pricePaid: double.tryParse(_pricePaidController.text) ?? 0,
        );

    return Scaffold(
      resizeToAvoidBottomInset: true,
